due to the incoming observations becoming less and less informative as
t
grows and thus no longer influencing the value of
\phi_{\ell|t}(\mathrm{Id})
.

The estimator proposed in
5.3
captures well the behavior of the asymptotic variance with little variance and also stagnates at the same time. We observed in our experiments that, in comparison with the variance estimators of filtering algorithms, for this estimator to provide good performance more samples are required for shorter time horizons. Nonetheless, the increased computational time incurred by the increase of the number of particles is to be compared with the time it takes to compute the crude variance estimates up to
t=160
, which is about
1
hour when running on GPU. In comparison, one run of our estimator takes
3
minutes.

Appendix A
Proofs

In this section we provide the proofs of Propositions
4.3
,
5.1
and Theorems
4.4
,
4.9
,
4.10
and
5.2
. The various Propositions and Lemmata used are stated and proved in Section
A.9
-
A.10
. Other intermediary technical results are provided in Section
C
. Equations, lemmata, propositions and theorems referred to without the prefix S are given in the main text.

A.1.
Preliminaries

In order to simplify the notations, in what follows we write

\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{0:t}\in[N]^{2(t+1)}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{1}_{0:t},k^{2}_{0:t})h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t}),

where

\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{1}_{0:t},k^{2}_{0:t}):=\prod_{s=0}^{t}N^{b_{s}}\bigg(\frac{N}{N-1}\bigg)^{1-b_{s}}\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}\mathrm{I}_{b,t}(k^{1}_{0:t},k^{2}_{0:t})\Lambda^{\mathsf{BS}}_{1,t}(k^{1}_{0:t})\Lambda^{\mathsf{BS}}_{2,t}(k^{1}_{0:t};k^{2}_{0:t}).

(S1.1)

By (
4.13
),

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\mathsf{BS}}_{1,t}(k^{1}_{0:t})

\displaystyle=\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\Lambda^{\mathsf{BS}}_{1,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1}),

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\mathsf{BS}}_{2,t}(k^{1}_{0:t};k^{2}_{0:t})

\displaystyle=\big\{\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{2}_{t-1})\mathbbm{1}_{k^{2}_{t}\neq k^{1}_{t}}+\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{2}_{t}=k^{1}_{t}}\big\}\Lambda^{\mathsf{BS}}_{2,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1};k^{2}_{0:t-1}).

and by (
3.4
) we have that
\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})=\gamma_{t-1}^{N}(\mathbf{1})N^{-1}\Omega_{t-1}
, hence, using (
4.16
)
\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)
becomes

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{0:t}\in[N]^{2(t+1)}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{1}_{0:t},k^{2}_{0:t})h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t})

(S1.2)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{2t}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\frac{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}{N^{2}}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\frac{N}{N-1}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{2}_{t-1})\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t},b_{t}=0}+N\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t},b_{t}=1}\bigg]h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t}).

A.2.
Proof of Proposition
4.3

Proof of Lemma
4.1
.

By
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:A})}}
, for any
(x,y)\in\mathsf{X}^{2}
,

\displaystyle\beta^{N}_{t}(x,y)\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}x)

\displaystyle=\beta^{N}_{t}(x,y)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathcal{W}^{i}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{i}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}x)\lx@nobreakspace

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{t-1}(y)m_{t}(y,x)}{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\omega^{i}_{t-1}m_{t}(\xi^{i}_{t-1},x)}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathcal{W}^{i}_{t-1}m_{t}(\xi^{i}_{t-1},x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)

\displaystyle=\frac{g_{t-1}(y)}{\Omega_{t-1}}\mathrm{M}_{t}(y,\mathrm{d}x).

Consequently, for any
(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{1}_{t})\in\mathsf{X}^{2}
and
h\in\mathbb{F}(\mathcal{X})
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\beta^{N}_{t}(\xi^{k_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k_{t-1}}_{t-1})h(\xi^{k_{t}}_{t})\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\big]

\displaystyle=\int\beta^{N}_{t}(x,\xi^{k_{t-1}}_{t-1})h(x)\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}x)

\displaystyle=\int\frac{1}{\Omega_{t-1}}\omega^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1}h(x)\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}x)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{W}^{k_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}[h](\xi^{k_{t-1}}_{t-1}).

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbbm{1}_{k_{t-1}=A^{k_{t}}_{t-1}}h(\xi^{k_{t}}_{t})\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\big]=\int\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbbm{1}_{k_{t-1}=i}\mathcal{W}^{i}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{i}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}x)h(x)=\mathcal{W}^{k_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}[h](\xi^{k_{t-1}}_{t-1}).

∎

Proof of Proposition
4.3
.

For the proof of i), note that conditionally on
\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}
,
\xi^{1:N}_{t}
are i.i.d. with distribution
\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}
, hence,

\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{2}_{t-1})h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\\
=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\int\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{2}_{t-1})h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t})\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t}),

and by (
4.11
) in Lemma
4.1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{2}_{t-1})h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\frac{N(N-1)}{\Omega_{t-1}^{2}}\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h]\big)(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1}),

(S1.3)

where

g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h]:(x_{0:t-1},x^{\prime}_{0:t-1})\mapsto g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}(x_{t-1},x^{\prime}_{t-1})\int h(x_{0:t},x^{\prime}_{0:t})\mathrm{M}_{t}(x_{t-1},\mathrm{d}x_{t})\mathrm{M}_{t}(x^{\prime}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{t}).

On the other hand, by (
4.11
) in Lemma
4.1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\int\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t})\delta_{\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t}}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\frac{1}{\Omega_{t-1}^{2}}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}}\omega^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\omega^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\int h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t})\delta_{\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t}}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\frac{N}{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h]\big)(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t}),

(S1.4)

where

g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h]:(x_{0:t-1},x^{\prime}_{0:t-1})\mapsto g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}(x_{t-1},x^{\prime}_{t-1})\int h(x_{0:t},x^{\prime}_{0:t})\mathrm{M}_{t}(x_{t-1},\mathrm{d}x_{t})\delta_{x_{t}}(\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{t}).

If
b_{t}=0
, since
\Omega_{t-1}
and
\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})
are
\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}
measurable for any
(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\in[N]^{2t}
, by (
S1.2
) and (
A.2
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\frac{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}{N(N-1)}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{2}_{t-1})h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h]\big)(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h]).

If
b_{t}=1
, again by (
S1.2
) and (
A.2
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\frac{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}{N}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}h(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h]\big)(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1},\xi^{k^{2}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h]\big).

For the proof of ii), we proceed by induction. Let
t=0
and
h\in\mathbb{F}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{2}})
. If
b_{0}=0
, since
\xi^{1:N}_{0}\overset{\mathrm{iid}}{\sim}\mathrm{M}_{0}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{0,0}(h)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{N(N-1)}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}h(\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{j}_{0})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N(N-1)}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{0}[h]=\mathcal{M}^{0}_{0}[h]=\mathcal{Q}_{b,0}(h).

If
b_{0}=1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{1,0}(h)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}h(\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{i}_{0})\right]=\mathcal{M}^{1}_{0}[h]=\mathcal{Q}_{b,0}(h).

Let
t\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and
h\in\mathbb{F}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{2(t+1)}})
. Assume that
\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(f)\big]=\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(f)
for any
b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}
and
f\in\mathbb{F}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{2t}})
.
By
(i)
in Proposition
4.3
and the tower property

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\big]\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h]\big)\right]

and
g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h]\in\mathbb{F}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{2t}})
. Thus, by the induction hypothesis and the definition of
\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}
we get

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\right]=\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h]\big)=\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}(h),

which completes the proof. The proof of iii) is a direct consequence of (
4.4
), (
4.17
) and ii).
∎

A.3.
Proof of Proposition
5.1

Let
h_{0:t}
be an additive functional (
5.5
). By definition, for
s\in[t]
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{\mathbf{G}}_{s,t}(x_{s},h_{0:t})

\displaystyle=\int\big\{\tilde{h}_{0:s}(x_{0:s})+\tilde{h}_{s:t}(x_{s:t})\big\}\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}(x_{s},\mathrm{d}x_{0:s-1})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s+1:t}(x_{s},\mathrm{d}x_{s+1:t})

\displaystyle=\int\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[\tilde{h}_{0:s}](x_{s})+\tilde{h}_{s:t}(x_{s:t})\big\}\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s+1:t}(x_{s},\mathrm{d}x_{s+1:t}),

and then, setting
\mathsf{H}_{s:t}:x_{s:t}\mapsto\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[\tilde{h}_{0:s}](x_{s})+\tilde{h}_{s:t}(x_{s:t})
we get

\displaystyle\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{G}}_{s,t}[h_{0:t}]^{2})

\displaystyle=\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s+1:t}\big[\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[\tilde{h}_{0:s}]+\tilde{h}_{s:t}\big]^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=\int\gamma_{0:s-1}(\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{0:s-1})g_{s-1}(x^{\prime}_{s-1})\int\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s+1:t}[\mathsf{H}_{s:t}](x_{s})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s+1:t}[\mathsf{H}_{s:t}](x_{s})\gamma_{0:s}(\mathrm{d}x_{0:s})

\displaystyle=\int\gamma_{0:s-1}(\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{0:s-1})g_{s-1}(x^{\prime}_{s-1})\gamma_{0:s}(\mathrm{d}x_{0:s})\delta_{x_{s}}(\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{s})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s+1:t}[\mathsf{H}_{s:t}](x_{s})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s+1:t}[\mathsf{H}_{s:t}](x^{\prime}_{s}),

which establishes the result since by definition

\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t}\big(\mathrm{d}x_{0:t},\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{0:t}\big)=\gamma_{0:s}(\mathrm{d}x_{0:s})\gamma_{0:s-1}(\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{0:s-1})g_{s-1}(x^{\prime}_{s-1})\delta_{x_{s}}(\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{s})\\
\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s+1:t}\big(x_{s},\mathrm{d}x_{s+1:t}\big)\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s+1:t}\big(x^{\prime}_{s},\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{s+1:t}\big).

If
s=0
, then
\mathrm{\mathbf{G}}_{0,t}[h_{0:t}](x_{0})=\int h_{0:t}(x_{0:t})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{1:t}(x_{0},\mathrm{d}x_{1:t})
and

\displaystyle\gamma_{0}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{0}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{G}}_{0:t}[h_{0:t}]^{2}\big)=\gamma_{0}(\mathrm{\mathbf{G}}_{0:t}[h_{0:t}]^{2})

\displaystyle=\int\mathrm{M}_{0}(\mathrm{d}x_{0})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{1:t}[h_{0:t}](x_{0})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{1:t}[h_{0:t}](x_{0})

\displaystyle=\int\mathrm{M}_{0}(\mathrm{d}x_{0})\delta_{x_{0}}(\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}_{0})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{1:t}[h_{0:t}](x_{0})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{1:t}[h_{0:t}](x^{\prime}_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}_{e_{0},t}(h_{0:t}^{\otimes 2}).

A.4.
Proof of Theorem
4.4

Let
m\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and
N\geq 2
. Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}

\displaystyle:=\{k^{1:2m}\in[N]^{2m}:k^{2i-1}\neq k^{2i},i\in[1:m]\},

(S1.5)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\{k^{1:2m}\in[N]^{2m}:k^{2i-1}=k^{2i},i\in[1:m]\}.

(S1.6)

Define also for any
p\in[2m]
,

\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}:=\{k^{1:2m}\in[N]^{2m}:\mathrm{Card}(\{k^{1},k^{2},\cdots,k^{2m-1},k^{2m}\})=p\}.

Then
[N]^{2m}=\bigsqcup_{p=1}^{2m}\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}
and

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}=\bigsqcup_{p=1}^{2m}\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}=\bigsqcup_{p=2}^{2m}\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m},\quad\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}=\bigsqcup_{p=1}^{2m}\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}=\bigsqcup_{p=1}^{m}\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m},

(S1.7)

where
\bigsqcup
means disjoint union.
The first equality holds because the tuples in
\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}
must contain at least two different values and the second because tuples in
\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}
contain at most
m
different values. The proof of Theorem
4.4
is concerned with
m=2
and that of Proposition
A.4
with
m\geq 2
. Example
A.1
provides the intersections for the case
m=2
.

Example A.1
.

Choose
m=2
and
N\geq 4
. Then,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{k^{1:4}\in[N]^{4}:k^{1}\neq k^{2},k^{3}\neq k^{4},\{k^{3},k^{4}\}=\{k^{1},k^{2}\}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{k^{1:4}\in[N]^{4}:k^{1}\neq k^{2},k^{3}\neq k^{4},k^{3}\in\{k^{1},k^{2}\},k^{4}\notin\{k^{1},k^{2}\}\},

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\sqcup\{k^{1:4}\in[N]^{4}:k^{1}\neq k^{2},k^{3}\neq k^{4},k^{3}\notin\{k^{1},k^{2}\},k^{4}\in\{k^{1},k^{2}\}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{k^{1:4}\in[N]^{4}:k^{1}\neq k^{2}\neq k^{3}\neq k^{4}\},

with
\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2})=2N(N-1)
,
\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2})=4N(N-1)(N-2)
and
\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2})=N(N-1)(N-2)(N-3)
. As for
\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{k^{1:4}\in[N]^{4}:k^{1}=k^{2}=k^{3}=k^{4}\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{k^{1:4}\in[N]^{4}:k^{1}=k^{2},k^{3}=k^{4},k^{1}\neq k^{3}\}.

with
\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2})=N
and
\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2})=N(N-1).

Proof of Theorem
4.4
.

We proceed by induction. Throughout the proof we assume that
N\geq 4
for the sake of simplicity. For
t=0
and
b=\mathbf{0}
, using (
4.23
) and (
4.20
),

\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)=N^{-1}(N-1)^{-1}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}h(\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{j}_{0}),

and
\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)=\mathrm{M}_{0}^{\otimes 2}(h)
.

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\big\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{1}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{i,j,i^{\prime},j^{\prime}\in[N]^{4}}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j,i^{\prime}\neq j^{\prime}}\big\{h(\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{j}_{0})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\big\}\big\{h(\xi^{i^{\prime}}_{0},\xi^{j^{\prime}}_{0})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\big\}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\frac{\tau_{0}+\overline{\tau}_{0}}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}},

where

\displaystyle\tau_{0}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i,j,i^{\prime},j^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}}\{h(\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{j}_{0})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\}\{h(\xi^{i^{\prime}}_{0},\xi^{j^{\prime}}_{0})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\}\right],

\displaystyle\overline{\tau}_{0}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i,j,i^{\prime},j^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\overline{\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}}}\{h(\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{j}_{0})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\}\{h(\xi^{i^{\prime}}_{0},\xi^{j^{\prime}}_{0})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\}\right],

where
\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}
is defined in (
S1.5
), (
S1.7
) and explicited in Example
A.1
, and
\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\overline{\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}}=(\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2})\sqcup(\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2})
.
If
(i,j,i^{\prime},j^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}
, then
\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{j}_{0},\xi^{i^{\prime}}_{0}
and
\xi^{j^{\prime}}_{0}
are i.i.d. Therefore,

\mathbb{E}\left[\big\{h(\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{j}_{0})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\big\}\big\{h(\xi^{i^{\prime}}_{0},\xi^{j^{\prime}}_{0})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\big\}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\big\{h(\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{j}_{0})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\big\}\right]^{2}=0,

and
\tau_{0}=0
. Hence, using the fact that because
h
is bounded,
\|h-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\|_{\infty}\leq 2\|h\|_{\infty}
and that
\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\overline{\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}})=4N(N-1)(N-2)+2N(N-1)
by Example
A.1
, we get

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},0}(h)\big\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\overline{\tau}_{0}}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\|h\|^{2}_{\infty}}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{i,j,i^{\prime},j^{\prime}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\overline{\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}}}1,

\displaystyle=\frac{4\|h\|^{2}_{\infty}\big[4N(N-1)(N-2)+2N(N-1)\big]}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}=\mathcal{O}(N^{-1}),

which completes the proof for
b=0
. For
b=1
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{1,0}(h)-\mathcal{Q}_{1,0}(h)=N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}h(\xi^{i}_{0},\xi^{i}_{0})-\int h(x,x)\mathrm{M}_{0}(\mathrm{d}x),

and since
h
is bounded,

\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{1,0}(h)-\mathcal{Q}_{1,0}(h)\big\|^{2}_{2}=N^{-1}\mathbb{V}_{\mathrm{M}_{0}}\big[h(\xi,\xi)\big]=\mathcal{O}(N^{-1}),

where
\mathbb{V}_{\mathrm{M}_{0}}
is the variance under
\mathrm{M}_{0}
. This completes the proof for
t=0
. Let
t>0
, and assume now that (
4.18
) holds at time
t-1
. Consider the following decomposition

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}(h)=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\big]-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}(h),

which, by Proposition
4.3
, becomes

\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}(h)=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\\
+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h]).

(S1.8)

By the induction hypothesis, since
h
is bounded and also
g_{t-1}
by
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B})}}
, we have

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\right\|_{2}=0,

hence, by Minkowski’s inequality it remains to prove that

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\right\|_{2}=0.

(S1.9)

By Proposition
4.3
,
\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\big]=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])
and

\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])^{2}\big],

hence,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|^{2}_{2}=\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big\|_{2}^{2}-\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}^{2}.

Consequently, by Proposition
A.8
, if
b_{t}=0
,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h])\right\|^{2}_{2}

(S1.10)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}-1\right)\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h])\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{N-2}{N-1}G_{\infty}^{3}|h|^{2}_{\infty}\int\nu(\mathrm{d}x)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\Omega_{t-1}}{N}\bigg\{\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes m_{t}(.,x))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.))+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes m_{t}(.,x))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.))\bigg]

\displaystyle+G_{\infty}^{2}|h|^{2}_{\infty}\int\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}y,\mathrm{d}x)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\Omega_{t-1}^{2}}{N(N-1)}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\bigg\{\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\right)\bigg\}\bigg],

and if
b_{t}=1
,

\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])\right\|^{2}_{2}\leq\left(\frac{N-1}{N}-1\right)\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])\|^{2}_{2}\\
+G_{\infty}^{3}|h|_{\infty}^{2}\int\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\Omega_{t-1}}{N}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})\right]\nu(\mathrm{d}x).

(S1.11)

By Proposition
A.4
, the first term in the r.h.s. of (
S1.10
) and (
S1.11
) is
\mathcal{O}(N^{-1})
in both cases because

\big|g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h]\big|_{\infty}\leq G_{\infty}^{2}|h|_{\infty}<\infty
. We now show that the remaining terms go to zero when
N
goes to infinity. Define for any
x\in\mathsf{X}
and
N\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
,

\displaystyle B_{N}(x)

\displaystyle:=\frac{\Omega_{t-1}}{N}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\right)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)),

(S1.12)

\displaystyle\widetilde{B}_{N}(x)

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\right)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1}\right),

\displaystyle\widetilde{B}(x)

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}\left(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\right)\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1}).

We apply Theorem
C.1
with
f_{N}=\mathbb{E}\big[B_{N}\big]
,
g_{N}=\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{B}_{N}\big]
,
g=\widetilde{B}
and
f=0
. To establish i),
note that
\mathbb{E}\big[B_{N}(x)\big]\leq G_{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{B}_{N}(x)\big]
for all
N\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and
x\in\mathsf{X}
, since for all
(x,i)\in\mathsf{X}\times[N]
,
\beta^{N}_{t}(x,\xi^{i}_{t-1})\leq 1
and
N^{-1}\Omega_{t-1}\leq G_{\infty}
. Then, to prove ii), for all
(h,f)\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{t}})^{2}
, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(h)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(f)-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(h)\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(f)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left(\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(h)-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(h)\right)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(f)\right|\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left(\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(f)-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(f)\right)\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(h)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(h)-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(h)\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(f)\big\|_{2}+\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(f)-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(f)\big\|_{2}\big|\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(h)\big|,

which goes to zero by the induction hypothesis, the fact that
\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(f)\big\|_{2}<\infty
by Proposition
A.4
and
|\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(h)|<\infty
. Hence, for all
x\in\mathsf{X}
,

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}g_{N}(x)=g(x)\quad\text{and}\quad\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{2}\right]=\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{2}.

Added to the fact that
\int\widetilde{B}_{N}(x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{2}
and
\int\widetilde{B}(x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{2}
, we get by applying Fubini’s theorem

\displaystyle\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\int\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{B}_{N}(x)\right]\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{2}\right]=\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{2}=\int\widetilde{B}(x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x).

Then, for iii), first we have that
\mathbb{E}\big[B_{N}(x)^{3/2}\big]\leq G_{\infty}^{3/2}\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{B}_{N}(x)^{3/2}]
and

\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{B}_{N}(x)^{3/2}]\leq\sigma_{+}^{3/2}\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{3}\big],

where the r.h.s. is finite by choosing
m=3
in Proposition
A.4
. The family of non negative random variables
\{B_{N}(x)\}_{N\in\mathbb{N}}
is then uniformly integrable for any
x\in\mathsf{X}
. Indeed, for any
x\in\mathsf{X}
,
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}^{*}_{+}
and
N\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[B_{N}(x)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{N}(x)\geq\alpha}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[B_{N}(x)^{3/2}\big]/\sqrt{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{+}^{3/2}G_{\infty}^{3/2}\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{3}\big]/\sqrt{\alpha},

hence
\underset{\alpha\to\infty}{\lim}\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[B_{N}(x)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{N}(x)\geq\alpha}\right]=0.

On the other hand,

B_{N}(x)=\frac{\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\right)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\mathbf{1}\otimes g_{t-1}m_{t}(.,x)\right)}{N\phi_{t-1}^{N}\left(m_{t}(.,x)\right)},

and the induction hypothesis coupled with the fact that
\phi_{t-1}^{N}(m_{t}(.,x))\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\phi_{t-1}(m_{t}(.,x))
with
\phi_{t-1}(m_{t}(.,x))>0
by
(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B}:\ref *{assp:positive}})
gives

\frac{\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\right)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\mathbf{1}\otimes g_{t-1}m_{t}(.,x)\right)}{\phi_{t-1}^{N}(m_{t}(.,x))}\\
\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\frac{\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}\left(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\right)\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}\left(\mathbf{1}\otimes g_{t-1}m_{t}(.,x)\right)}{\phi_{t-1}(m_{t}(.,x))}.

Hence,
B_{N}(x)\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}0
, and by uniform integrability, for any
x\in\mathsf{X}

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}f_{N}(x)=\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}[B_{N}(x)]=0.

Finally, by Theorem
C.1
we deduce that

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\int\mathbb{E}\big[B_{N}(x)\big]\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=\int\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\big[B_{N}(x)\big]\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=0.

(S1.13)

The other similar terms are treated in the same way by adapting the definitions in (
S1.12
). As for the second integral, define for any
(x,y)\in\mathsf{X}^{2}
,

\displaystyle R_{N}(x,y)

\displaystyle:=\frac{\Omega_{t-1}^{2}}{N(N-1)}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)).

Then, using that

\int\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y)\big)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\right)\nu(\mathrm{d}y)\\
\leq\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\big)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1}\right),

together with Fubini’s theorem we obtain, using
N\geq 4
,

0\leq\int\mathbb{E}\big[R_{N}(x,y)\big]\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}x,\mathrm{d}y)\leq\frac{4G_{\infty}}{3}\int\mathbb{E}\big[B_{N}(x)\big]\nu(\mathrm{d}x),

and by (
S1.13
) we get that

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\int\mathbb{E}\big[R_{N}(x,y)\big]\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}x,\mathrm{d}y)=0.

The remaining term goes to zero by a similar reasoning. This completes the proof of (
4.18
).

For the convergence rate, by the strong mixing assumption we have that

\beta^{N}_{t}(x,y)\leq\frac{G_{\infty}\sigma_{+}}{\sigma_{-}\Omega_{t-1}}\quad\forall(x,y)\in\mathsf{X}^{2},

(S1.14)

and in the case
b_{t}=0
, we have for example that

\displaystyle\int\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})\nu(\mathrm{d}x)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}\sigma_{+}}{\sigma_{-}\Omega_{t-1}}\int\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})\nu(\mathrm{d}x)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}\sigma_{+}}{\sigma_{-}\Omega_{t-1}}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{2}.

and

\displaystyle\int\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y)\big)

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\big)\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}x,\mathrm{d}y)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}\sigma^{2}_{+}}{\sigma^{2}_{-}\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})^{2}.

Thus, replacing in
(i)
, we get

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h])\big\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\left[\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}-1\right]\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\frac{2\sigma_{+}G_{\infty}^{4}|h|^{2}_{\infty}}{\sigma_{-}}\left[\frac{2(N-2)}{N(N-1)}+\frac{\sigma_{+}}{\sigma_{-}N(N-1)}\right]\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})\big\|^{2}_{2}.

The case
b_{t}=1
is handled similarly using
(ii)
which yields

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])\big\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\left[\frac{N-1}{N}-1\right]\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{\sigma_{+}G_{\infty}^{4}|h|_{\infty}^{2}}{N}\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})\big\|^{2}_{2}.

Both upper bounds are
\mathcal{O}(N^{-1})
by Proposition
A.4
. This concludes the proof.
∎

A.5.
Proof of Theorem
4.7

The proof is a straightforward adaptation of the proof in
[
28
, Theorem 1]
. Let
h\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X})
. By (
4.30
),

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\gamma,t}(h)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left(\frac{N-1}{N}\right)^{t}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}(h^{\otimes 2}_{t})+N\left[\left(\frac{N-1}{N}\right)^{t+1}-1\right]\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\mathbf{0},t}(h^{\otimes 2}_{t})

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}\setminus\{\mathbf{0},e_{0:t}\}}N\bigg\{\prod_{s=0}^{t}\frac{1}{N^{b_{s}}}\bigg(\frac{N-1}{N}\bigg)^{1-b_{s}}\bigg\}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h^{\otimes 2}_{t})

\displaystyle\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left\{\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}(h^{\otimes 2}_{t})-\mathcal{Q}_{\mathbf{0},t}(h^{\otimes 2}_{t})\right\}=\mathcal{V}^{\infty}_{\gamma,t}(h),

where we have used that for any
b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}
,
\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h^{\otimes 2}_{t})\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}(h^{\otimes 2}_{t})
by Theorem
4.4
.

A.6.
Proof of Theorem
4.9

Define for any
t\in[N]
and
b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}
,

\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t}:=\sigma\big(\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\cup\sigma(\{J^{i}_{k,t-1}\}_{(i,k)\in[N]^{2}})\cup\sigma(\{A^{i}_{t-1},\xi^{i}_{t}\}_{i=1}^{N})\big),

(S1.15)

with
\mathcal{G}^{N}_{0}=\mathcal{F}^{N}_{0}
. In the following, we write

\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}:=\left\{\prod_{s=0}^{t}N^{b_{s}}\left(\frac{N}{N-1}\right)^{1-b_{s}}\right\}\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}/N^{2}.

(S1.16)

The intermediary results used in the next proof are given in Section
A.10
.

Proof of Theorem
4.9
.

Let
h\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{2}})
. We proceed again by induction. The case
t=0
is a consequence of Theorem
4.4
since
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,0}(h)=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,0}(h)
for any
b\in\mathcal{B}_{0}
. Let
t>0
. Similarly to Theorem
4.4
we make use of the following decomposition:

\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}(h)=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\\
+\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])-\mathcal{Q}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h]).

By Minkowski’s inequality and the induction hypothesis, it remains to prove that

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}=0.

(S1.17)

By Lemma
A.9
,
\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])
and

\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])^{2}\big],

hence,

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|^{2}_{2}=\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)\big\|_{2}^{2}-\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}^{2}.

By Proposition
A.10
, if
b_{t}=0
,

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=2}^{4}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+|h|_{\infty}^{2}\sum_{p=2}^{3}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg],

and

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\left(\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}-1\right)\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+|h|_{\infty}^{2}\sum_{p=2}^{3}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg].

By Proposition
A.12
,
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B})}}
and the fact that
h
is bounded,
\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}^{2}<\infty
and

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\left(\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}-1\right)\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}^{2}=0,

and by
(i)
in Proposition
A.11
, the second term in the r.h.s. also goes to zero, which shows (
S1.17
) when
b_{t}=0
. If
b_{t}=1
,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N-1}{N}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}^{2}+|h|_{\infty}^{2}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg],

and
\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)-\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])\big\|^{2}_{2}
goes to zero similarly to the case
b_{t}=0
and by application of Proposition
A.11
.

The convergence rate follows straightforwardly by Proposition
A.11
since for
p\in\{2,3\}

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]=\mathcal{O}(N^{-1}),

and

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]=\mathcal{O}(N^{-1}).

∎

A.7.
Proof of Theorem
4.10

The proof boils down to showing a
PaRIS
version of the identity (
4.30
). Let us first prove that for all
t\in\mathbb{N}
and
(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\in[N]^{2}
,

\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})=1.

(S1.18)

We proceed by induction. If
t=0
,

\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{0}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{0},k^{2}_{0})=\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{0}\neq k^{2}_{0}}+\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{0}=k^{2}_{0}}=1.

Let
t>0
and assume that (
S1.18
) holds at
t-1
for all
(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\in[N]^{2}
. By the induction hypothesis,

\displaystyle\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})

\displaystyle=\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{t-1}}M^{-1}\bigg\{\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1})+\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{W}^{n}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},n)\bigg\}

\displaystyle=M^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\big\{\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}}\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{t-1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1})+\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathcal{W}^{n}_{t-1}\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{t-1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},n)\bigg\}

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}}M^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M}1+\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}}M^{-1}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\mathcal{W}^{n}_{t-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}}+\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}}=1.

which proves (
S1.18
) at time
t
. Consequently, we have that for all
h\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X})

\displaystyle\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}}\prod_{s=0}^{t}N^{-b_{s}}\left(\frac{N-1}{N}\right)^{1-b_{s}}\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h^{\otimes 2})

\displaystyle=\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}}\frac{\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}{N^{2}}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}{N^{2}}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})

\displaystyle=\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}\eta_{t}^{N}(h)^{2}=\gamma_{t}^{N}(h)^{2}.

The convergence in probability is then obtained by mimicking the proof of Theorem
4.7
and using Theorem
4.9
.

A.8.
Proof of Theorem
5.2

The proof of Theorem
5.2
requires the convergence of
\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](x)
to
\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](x)

\mathbb{P}
-a.s. for any
x\in\mathsf{X}
.

Proposition A.2
.

For any
s\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
, any
x\in\mathsf{X}
and additive functional
h_{0:s}
(
5.5
),

\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](x)\overset{a.s.}{\underset{N\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}}\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](x).

(S1.19)

Proof.

Let
x\lx@nobreakspace\in\mathsf{X}
. Define

\displaystyle\begin{cases}a_{N}:=\eta_{s-1}^{N}\left(g_{s-1}\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s-1}^{N}[\tilde{h}_{0:s-1}]f^{x}_{s-1}+\tilde{f}^{x}_{s-1}\big\}\right),\\
b_{N}:=\eta_{s-1}^{N}\left(g_{s-1}m_{s}(.,x)\right),\\
b:=\eta_{s-1}\big(g_{s-1}m_{s}(.,x)\big).\end{cases}

where
f^{x}_{s-1}:y\mapsto m_{s}(y,x)
and

\tilde{f}^{x}_{s-1}:y\mapsto m_{s}(y,x)\left\{\tilde{h}_{s-1}(y,x)-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](x)\right\}.

Then, we have that
a_{N}/b_{N}=\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](x)-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](x)
. By
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:boundup})}}
,

(f^{x}_{s-1},\tilde{f}^{x}_{s-1})\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X})^{2}
for any
x\in\mathsf{X}
and

\displaystyle\eta_{s-1}\left(g_{s-1}\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s-1}[\tilde{h}_{0:s-1}]f^{x}_{s-1}+\tilde{f}^{x}_{s-1}\}\right)

\displaystyle=0.

Hence, choosing
f_{s-1}=f^{x}_{s-1}
and
\tilde{f}_{s-1}=\tilde{f}^{x}_{s-1}
in Theorem
C.2
(
S3.1
), there exists
(d,\tilde{d})\in(\mathbb{R}^{*}_{+})^{2}
such that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|a_{N}\right|\geq\epsilon\right)\leq\tilde{d}\exp(-dN\epsilon^{2})\lx@nobreakspace.

(S1.20)

On the other hand, by choosing
f_{s-1}=f^{x}_{s-1}
and
\tilde{f}_{s-1}=0
, there exists
(d^{\prime},\tilde{d}^{\prime})\in(\mathbb{R}^{*}_{+})^{2}
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|b_{N}-b\right|\geq\epsilon\right)\leq\tilde{d}^{\prime}\exp(-d^{\prime}N\epsilon^{2}).

Finally, since
\left|a_{N}/b_{N}\right|\leq\left|\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](x)\right|+\left|\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](x)\right|\leq 2|h_{0:s}|_{\infty}

\mathbb{P}
-a.s. and
b>0
by
(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B}:\ref *{assp:positive}})
, there exist
(c_{s},\tilde{c}_{s})\in(\mathbb{R}^{*}_{+})^{2}
by Lemma
C.3
such that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|a_{N}/b_{N}\right|\geq\epsilon\right)=\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](x)-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](x)\right|\geq\epsilon\right)\leq\tilde{c}_{s}\exp(-c_{s}N\epsilon^{2}),

from which (
S1.19
) follows by applying the Borel-Cantelli Lemma.
∎

Proposition A.3
.

For any
s\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and additive functional
h_{0:s}
(
5.5
)

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{s-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{s}\left[\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}]\big)^{4}\right]\right]=0.

(S1.21)

Proof.

The proof is a straightforward application of
[
33
, Lemma 17]
(which dates back to
[
15
]
). We recall it with its proof for the sake of completeness. Define for any
x\in\mathsf{X}

\displaystyle\begin{cases}A_{N}(x)&:=\big|\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](x)-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](x)\big|^{4}\phi_{s-1}^{N}\big(m_{s}(.,x)\big),\\
\widetilde{A}_{N}(x)&:=\phi_{s-1}^{N}\big(m_{s}(.,x)\big),\\
\widetilde{A}(x)&:=\phi_{s-1}\big(m_{s}(.,x)\big).\end{cases}

We apply Theorem
C.1
with
f_{N}=\mathbb{E}\big[A_{N}\big]
,
g_{N}=\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{A}_{N}\big]
,
f=0
and
g=\widetilde{A}
.

(i)

For any
x_{s}\in\mathsf{X}
,
\big|\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]\big|(x_{s})\leq\int|h_{0:s}|(x_{0:s})\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}(x_{s},\mathrm{d}x_{0:s-1})\leq|h_{0:s}|_{\infty}
, hence

\mathbb{E}\big[A_{N}(x)\big]\leq 16|h_{0:s}|^{4}_{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{A}_{N}(x)\big].

(ii)

We have that
\widetilde{A}_{N}(x)\overset{a.s.}{\underset{N\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}}\widetilde{A}(x)
for any
x\in\mathsf{X}
by
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:boundup})}}
, (
3.5
) and by the dominated convergence theorem
\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{A}_{N}(x)\big]=\widetilde{A}(x)
. On the other hand,
\int\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{A}_{N}(x)\big]\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=\mathbb{E}\big[\phi_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\big]
,
\int\widetilde{A}(x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=\phi_{s}(\mathbf{1})
and
\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\big[\phi_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\big]=\phi_{s}(\mathbf{1})
again by the dominated convergence theorem. Hence

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\int\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{A}_{N}(x)\big]\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\big[\phi_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\big]=\phi_{s}(\mathbf{1})=\int\widetilde{A}(x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x).

(S1.22)

(iii)

By Proposition
A.2
,
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:boundup})}}
and (
3.5
)

A_{N}(x)\overset{a.s.}{\underset{N\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}}0,

and since
A_{N}(x)\leq 16|h_{0:s}|^{4}_{\infty}\sigma_{+}

\mathbb{P}
-a.s., by the dominated convergence theorem we get
\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\big[A_{N}(x)]=0.

Finally, by Theorem
C.1

\displaystyle\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\int A_{N}(x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)\bigg]

\displaystyle=\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\big[\phi_{s-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{s}\big[\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}]\big)^{4}\big]\big]

(S1.23)

\displaystyle=\int\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\big[A_{N}(x)\big]\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=0.

∎

Proof of Theorem
5.2
.

We write

\displaystyle\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]c_{t}+\tilde{h}_{s:t},\quad\textbf{H}_{s,t}:=\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}]d_{t}+\tilde{h}_{s:t}

\displaystyle\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[f_{0:s}]c_{t}+\tilde{f}_{s:t},\quad\textbf{F}_{s,t}:=\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[f_{0:s}]d_{t}+\tilde{f}_{s:t}

We proceed by induction on
t\geq s
with
s
fixed. By Theorem
4.4
,

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},s}\big(\textbf{H}_{s,s}\otimes\textbf{F}_{s,s}\big)-\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},s}\big(\textbf{H}_{s,s}\otimes\textbf{F}_{s,s}\big)\right\|_{2}^{2}=0.

Hence, by the triangle inequality it suffices to show that the difference with the ”idealized”
estimator goes to 0, i.e.

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},s}\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,s}\big)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},s}\big(\textbf{H}_{s,s}\otimes\textbf{F}_{s,s}\big)\right\|_{2}^{2}=0.

(S1.24)

For any
(h_{0:s},f_{0:s})
(
5.5
) and
(h_{s},f_{s})\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X})^{2}
, by (
4.20
)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},s}\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,s}\big)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},s}\big(\textbf{H}_{s,s}\otimes\textbf{F}_{s,s}\big)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq N^{-2}\bigg\|\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\frac{N^{s+1}\gamma_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}{(N-1)^{s}}\mathcal{T}^{e_{s}}_{s}(i,j)\bigg[\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{j}_{s})-\textbf{H}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\textbf{F}_{s,s}(\xi^{j}_{s})\bigg]\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq N^{-1}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\left\|\frac{N^{s}\gamma_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}{(N-1)^{s}}\mathcal{T}^{e_{s}}_{s}(i,j)\right\|_{4}\bigg\|\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{j}_{s})-\textbf{H}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\textbf{F}_{s,s}(\xi^{j}_{s})\bigg\|_{4}

by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. We now show
\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\left\|N^{s}(N-1)^{-s}\gamma_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}\mathcal{T}^{e_{s}}_{s}(i,j)\right\|_{4}
is bounded for all
(i,j)\in[N]^{2}
. We first show by induction that for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
\mathcal{T}^{\mathbf{0}}_{n}(i,j)\leq\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}
. For all
(i,j)\lx@nobreakspace\in[N]^{2}
,
\mathcal{T}^{\mathbf{0}}_{0}(i,j)=\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}
, and for any
n>0
, by (
4.23
)

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\mathbf{0}}_{n}(i,j)

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\sum_{k,\ell\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{n}(i,k)\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{n}(j,\ell)\mathcal{T}^{\mathbf{0}}_{n-1}(k,\ell)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\sum_{k,\ell\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{n}(i,k)\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{n}(j,\ell)\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\sum_{k,\ell\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{n}(i,k)\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{n}(j,\ell)\leq\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j},

where we have used the induction hypothesis in the second line. This shows the result. Next, we have that

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{e_{s}}_{s}(i,j)

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\sum_{k,\ell\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{s}(i,k)\mathcal{W}^{\ell}_{s-1}\mathcal{T}^{\mathbf{0}}_{s-1}(k,\ell)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\sum_{k,\ell\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{s}(i,k)\mathcal{W}^{\ell}_{s-1}\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\sum_{k,\ell\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{s}(i,k)\mathcal{W}^{\ell}_{s-1}=\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}.

Hence,
\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\left\|N^{s}\gamma_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}/(N-1)^{s}\mathcal{T}^{e_{s}}_{s}(i,j)\right\|_{4}\leq(2G_{\infty}^{2})^{s}\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}
. Consequently,

\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},s}\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,s}\big)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\textbf{H}_{s,s}\otimes\textbf{F}_{s,s}\big)\right\|_{2}\\
\leq\frac{(2G_{\infty}^{2})^{s}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})-\textbf{H}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\textbf{F}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\right\|_{4},

(S1.25)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{j}_{s})-\textbf{H}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\textbf{F}_{s,s}(\xi^{j}_{s})\right\|_{4}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\big\|\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})-\textbf{H}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\big)\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\big\|_{4}+\big\|\big(\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})-\textbf{F}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\big)\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\big\|_{4}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq C_{f,c}\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](\xi^{i}_{s})-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](\xi^{i}_{s})\big\|_{4}+C_{h,d}\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[f_{0:s}](\xi^{i}_{s})-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[f_{0:s}](\xi^{i}_{s})\big\|_{4}.

where
C_{f,d}:=|d_{s}|^{4}_{\infty}\big(|f_{0:s}|_{\infty}+|f_{s}|_{\infty}\big)^{4}
and
C_{h,c}:=|c_{s}|^{4}_{\infty}\big(|h_{0:s}|_{\infty}+|h_{s}|_{\infty}\big)^{4}

which are finite because
\tilde{h}_{s},\tilde{f}_{s})\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{2}})^{4}
,
(c_{s},d_{s},h_{s},f_{s})\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X})^{4}
. We have used that

\big|\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}(\xi^{i}_{s})\big|\leq\int|c_{s}|_{\infty}|h_{0:s}|_{\infty}\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}(\xi^{i}_{s},\mathrm{d}x_{0:s-1})+|h_{s}|_{\infty}=|c_{s}|_{\infty}|h_{0:s}|_{\infty}+|h_{s}|_{\infty}=C_{h,c}.

For any
h_{0:s}\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{s+1}})
,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](\xi^{i}_{s})-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](\xi^{i}_{s})\big\|_{4}^{4}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\big[\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](\xi^{i}_{s})-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](\xi^{i}_{s})\big)^{4}\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\big]\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathcal{W}^{j}_{s-1}\int\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](x)-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}](x)\big)^{4}\mathrm{M}_{s}(\xi^{j}_{s-1},\mathrm{d}x)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{s-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{s}\left[\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}]\big)^{4}\right]\right],

and replacing in (
S1.25
) we get

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,s}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},s}\big(\textbf{H}_{s,t}\otimes\textbf{F}_{s,t}\big)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq(2G_{\infty}^{2})^{s}\bigg\{C_{f,d}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{s-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{s}\left[\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}]\big)^{4}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt+C_{h,c}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{s-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{s}\left[\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[f_{0:s}]-\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[f_{0:s}]\big)^{4}\right]\right]\bigg\}

The upperbound goes to zero by Proposition
A.3
and this finishes the proof of the initialization.

Let
t>s
and
h_{0:s}\in\mathbb{A}_{b}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes s+1})
an additive functional. Assume that (
S1.24
) holds at
t-1
. By the induction hypothesis

\displaystyle\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)-\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}_{s,t}\big)\big\|_{2}=0,

where
\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}
is defined in (
3.1
) and for example

\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}(x_{s:t-1})=\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}](x_{s})\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}[c_{t}](x_{t-1})+\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}[\tilde{h}_{s:t}](x_{s:t-1}),

where
\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}[c_{t}]
and
\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}[\tilde{h}_{s:t}]
are bounded by
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B})}}
,
and by defintion of
\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t}
(
3.1
)

\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}_{s,t}\big)=\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t}\big(\textbf{H}_{s,t}\otimes\textbf{F}_{s,t}\big).

Hence, to prove (
5.10
) it is enough to show

\displaystyle\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)\big\|_{2}=0.

(S1.26)

Because
\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]
and
\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[f_{0:s}]
are
\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}
-measurable, by Proposition
4.3

\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\big]=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}),

and thus

\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)\big\|_{2}\\
=\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)\big\|_{2}-\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)\big\|_{2}.

(S1.27)

Now note that Proposition
A.8
is still applicable with
h=\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}
although there is a slight abuse because this specific
h
depends on the particles up to
s-1
through
\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]
and
\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[f_{0:s}]
. However, as they are
\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}
-measurable, Proposition
A.7
is still valid and hence Proposition
A.8
. Additionally, this specific
h
is bounded almost surely since for any
(x_{s:t},x^{\prime}_{s:t})\in\big(\mathsf{X}^{t-s+1}\big)^{2}

\big|\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}(x_{s:t})\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}(x^{\prime}_{s:t})\big|\leq C:=\big(|h_{0:s}|_{\infty}|c_{t}|_{\infty}+|\tilde{h}_{s:t}|_{\infty}\big)\big(|f_{0:s}|_{\infty}|d_{t}|_{\infty}+|\tilde{f}_{s:t}|_{\infty}\big)

and hence

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)-\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt\leq\left[\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}-1\right]\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}\big)\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{N-2}{N-1}G_{\infty}^{3}C^{2}\int\nu(\mathrm{d}x)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\Omega_{t-1}}{N}\bigg\{\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes m_{t}(.,x))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.))+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes m_{t}(.,x))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.))\bigg]

\displaystyle+\int\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}y,\mathrm{d}x)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}C^{2}\Omega_{t-1}^{2}}{N(N-1)}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\bigg\{\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\right)\bigg\}\bigg].

The first term in the r.h.s. goes to zero by Proposition
A.4
and the fact that
\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{H}^{N}_{s,t}\otimes\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\textbf{F}^{N}_{s,t}
are bounded. The remaining terms are similar to those that appear in the proof of Theorem
4.4
up to some constants and thus go to zero.

For the second part, by Theorem
C.2
and Borel-Cantelli Lemma,
\phi_{0:t|t}^{N}(h)\overset{a.s.}{\underset{N\to\infty}{\longrightarrow}}\phi_{0:t|t}(h)
. Then, by multiple applications of Theorem
5.2
and using the bilinearity of
\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}
and
\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}
, for any
s\in[0:t]
and bounded additive functional
h_{t}

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\big[

\displaystyle g_{t}\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]+\tilde{h}_{s:t}-\phi_{0:t|t}^{N}(h_{t})\big\}\big]^{\otimes 2}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\big[g_{t}\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]+\tilde{h}_{s:t}\big\}]^{\otimes 2}\big)-\phi_{0:t|t}^{N}(h_{t})\bigg(\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\big[g_{t}\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]+\tilde{h}_{s:t}\big\}]\otimes\mathbf{1}\big)+

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\mathbf{1}\otimes\big[g_{t}\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{0:s}]+\tilde{h}_{s:t}\big\}]\big)\bigg)+\phi_{0:t|t}^{N}(h_{t})^{2}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{e_{s},t}\big(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1}\big)

\displaystyle\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t}\big(\big[g_{t}\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}]+\tilde{h}_{s:t}\big\}]^{\otimes 2}\big)-\phi_{0:t|t}(h_{t})\bigg(\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t}\big(\big[g_{t}\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}]+\tilde{h}_{s:t}\big\}]\otimes\mathbf{1}\big)+

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t}\big(\mathbf{1}\otimes\big[g_{t}\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}]+\tilde{h}_{s:t}\big\}]\big)\bigg)+\phi_{0:t|t}(h_{t})^{2}\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t}\big(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}_{e_{s},t}\big(\big[g_{t}\big\{\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{0:s}]+\tilde{h}_{s:t}-\phi_{0:t|t}(h_{t})\big\}\big]^{\otimes 2}\big),

from which the weak consistency of
\mathcal{V}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{0:t|t}(h)
follows.
∎

A.9.
Supporting results for Theorem
4.4

In this section we prove Proposition
A.4
and the upperbound of
\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big\|^{2}_{2}
used in the proof of Theorem
4.4
.

Proposition A.4
.

Assume that
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B})}}
holds. For any
t\in\mathbb{N},

b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}
and
m\in\mathbb{N}
,

\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbb{E}\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})\big\|_{m}<\infty.

(S1.28)

We preface the proof with supporting lemmata.

Lemma A.5
.

For any
p\geq 2
, and
N\geq 2m

\displaystyle\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m})=\mathcal{O}(N^{p})\quad\textrm{and}\quad\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m})=\mathcal{O}(N^{p}).

Proof.

The tuples in
\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}
contain
p
distinct elements. These
p
distinct elements can be selected in
{N\choose p}
ways. For each of these tuples of size
p
, there are
p^{2m}
tuples of size
2m
with each element taking one of the
p
values. These tuples of size
2m
contain at most
p
distinct elements. Hence,

\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m})\leq\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m})\leq{N\choose p}p^{2m}\leq\frac{N^{p}}{p!}p^{2m},

and similarly,

\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m})\leq\frac{N^{p}}{p!}p^{2m}.

∎

Proposition A.6
.

Let
t\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
,
m\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and
(k^{1}_{t-1},\cdots,k^{2m}_{t-1})\in[N]^{2m}
.

(i)

If
p\in[2:2m]
and
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{2m}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}}.

(ii)

If
p\in[1:m]
and
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{2m}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{p+m}}{\Omega^{p+m}_{t-1}}.

Proof.

Let
p\in[2:2m]
. By definition there are
p
distinct elements in each
\bm{k}:=(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{2m}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}
. Let
\bm{k}_{p}:=\{a_{1},\cdots,a_{p}\}=\{k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{2m}_{t}\}
the set of cardinal
p
containing the
p
distinct elements in a tuple
\bm{k}\in\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}
. Define for any
a_{i}\in\bm{k}_{p}
,
V_{a_{i}}:=\{j\in[2m]:k^{j}_{t}=a_{i}\}
. Each
V_{a_{i}}
is non-empty so that it is possible to pick
j_{i}\in V_{a_{i}}
, and by (
4.11
) in Lemma
4.1
and the fact that different particles are i.i.d. conditionally to
\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt=\prod_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{j\in V_{a_{i}}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt=\prod_{i=1}^{p}\int\left\{\prod_{j\in V_{a_{i}}}\beta^{N}_{t}(\xi^{k^{j}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{j}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{a_{i}}_{t})\right\},

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt=\prod_{i=1}^{p}\int\left\{\prod_{j\in V_{a_{i}}\setminus\{j_{i}\}}\beta^{N}_{t}(\xi^{a_{i}}_{t},\xi^{k^{j}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\beta^{N}_{t}(\xi^{a_{i}}_{t},\xi^{k^{j_{i}}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{a_{i}}_{t})\right\},

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt=\prod_{i=1}^{p}\int\left\{\prod_{j\in V_{a_{i}}\setminus\{j_{i}\}}\beta^{N}_{t}(\xi^{a_{i}}_{t},\xi^{k^{j}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{j_{i}}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{k^{j_{i}}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}\xi^{a_{i}}_{t})\right\},

with the convention
\prod_{\emptyset}=1
.
Then, since for any
(k,\ell)\in[N]^{2}
,
\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t-1})\leq 1
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{m}_{t},k^{m}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\leq\prod_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\omega^{k^{j_{i}}_{t-1}}_{t-1}}{\Omega_{t-1}}\int\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{k^{j_{i}}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}\xi^{a_{i}}_{t})\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega_{t-1}^{p}}.

Now let
p\in[1:m]
and
\bm{k}=(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{2m}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}
. Define
\widetilde{V}_{a_{i}}:=V_{a_{i}}\cap\{1,3,\ldots,2m-1\}
for any
a_{i}\in\bm{k}_{p}
. These sets are non-empty since each
V_{a_{i}}
is non-empty and has as many even indices as odd indices, by definition of
\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}
. It is then possible to pick
j_{i}\in\widetilde{V}_{a_{i}}
and, since for any
i\in[N]

\mathcal{W}^{i}_{t-1}
is
\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}
-measurable,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\prod_{j=1}^{m}\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right],

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\prod_{j=1}^{m}\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\prod_{i=1}^{p}\int\left\{\prod_{j\in\widetilde{V}_{a_{i}}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{a_{i}}_{t})\right\},

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\prod_{j=1}^{m}\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\prod_{i=1}^{p}\int\left\{\prod_{j\in\widetilde{V}_{a_{i}}\setminus\{j_{i}\}}\beta^{N}_{t}(\xi^{a_{i}}_{t},\xi^{k^{j}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\beta^{N}_{t}(\xi^{a_{i}}_{t},\xi^{k^{j_{i}}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{a_{i}}_{t})\right\},

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\prod_{j=1}^{m}\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\prod_{i=1}^{p}\int\left\{\prod_{j\in\widetilde{V}_{a^{i}}\setminus\{j_{i}\}}\beta^{N}_{t}(\xi^{a_{i}}_{t},\xi^{k^{j}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{j_{i}}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{k^{j_{i}}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}\xi^{a_{i}}_{t})\right\},

with the convention
\prod_{\emptyset}=1
. Hence,

\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{p+m}}{\Omega^{p+m}_{t-1}}.

∎

Proof of proposition
A.4
.

Let
m\in\mathbb{N}
and assume for now that
N\geq 2m
.
We proceed by induction. For
t=0
, and
b_{0}=0
we have that
\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\mathbf{0},0}(\mathbf{1})=N^{-1}(N-1)^{-1}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}=1
which completes the proof. If
b_{0}=1
,
\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{1,0}(\mathbf{1})=N^{-1}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}=1
and the result follows.

Let
t\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and
b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}
. Assume (
S1.28
) holds at time
t-1
. Again we treat the cases
b_{t}=0
and
b_{t}=1
separately.
In the case
b_{t}=0
, by (
S1.2
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})^{m}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{0:t}\in[N]^{2m(t+1)}}\prod_{j=1}^{m}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{2j-1}_{0:t},k^{2j}_{0:t})\right]

and

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{0:t}\in[N]^{2m(t+1)}}\prod_{j=1}^{m}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{2j-1}_{0:t},k^{2j}_{0:t})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{2mt}}\prod_{j=1}^{m}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{2j-1}_{0:t-1},k^{2j}_{0:t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{t}\in[N]^{2m}}\prod_{j=1}^{m}\frac{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{2j-1}_{t}\neq k^{2j}_{t}}}{N(N-1)}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j}_{t},k^{2j}_{t-1}).

By Proposition
A.6
(i)
and Lemma
A.5
, for any
k^{1:2m}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2m}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{t}\in[N]^{2m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}(k^{2j}_{t},k^{2j}_{t-1})\mathbbm{1}_{k^{2j-1}_{t}\neq k^{2j}_{t}}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\sum_{p=2}^{2m}\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\sum_{p=2}^{2m}\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}}\frac{G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}}\leq\sum_{p=2}^{2m}\frac{G_{\infty}^{p}\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m})}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}}\leq C\sum_{p=2}^{2m}\frac{N^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}},

where
C
is a constant independent of
N
.
Consequently, using the fact that

\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{2mt}}\prod_{j=1}^{m}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{2j-1}_{0:t-1},k^{2j}_{0:t-1})=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})^{m}

which is
\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}
-measurable and that
\Omega_{t-1}\leq NG_{\infty}

\mathbb{P}
-a.s. by
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B})}}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})^{m}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})^{m}\frac{N^{2m-p}N^{p}}{N^{m}(N-1)^{m}}\right]\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})^{m}\right],

which completes the proof.
In the case
b_{t}=1
, again by (
S1.2
),

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{0:t}\in[N]^{2m(t+1)}}\prod_{j=1}^{m}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{2j-1}_{0:t},k^{2j}_{0:t})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{2mt}}\prod_{j=1}^{m}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{2j-1}_{0:t-1},k^{2j}_{0:t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times\sum_{k^{1:2m}_{t}\in[N]^{2m}}\prod_{j=1}^{m}\frac{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{2j-1}_{t}=k^{2j}_{t}}}{N}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}.

Then, similarly to the case
b_{t}=0
, by Proposition
A.6
-
(ii)
and Lemma
A.5
, for any
k^{1:2m}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2m}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{t}^{1:2m}\in[N]^{2m}}\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\prod_{j=1}^{m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{2j-1}_{t}=k^{2j}_{t}}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=1}^{m}\sum_{k_{t}^{1:2m}\in\mathcal{I}^{1}_{m}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{m}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=1}^{m}\sum_{k_{t}^{1:2m}\in\mathcal{I}^{1}_{m}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{m}}\frac{G_{\infty}^{m+p}}{\Omega^{m+p}_{t-1}}\leq C\sum_{p=1}^{m}\frac{N^{p}}{\Omega^{m+p}_{t-1}},

where
C
is a constant independent of
N
, and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})^{m}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})^{m}\sum_{p=1}^{m}\frac{\Omega^{2m}_{t-1}N^{p}}{N^{m}\Omega^{m+p}}\right]\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})^{m}\right],

which completes the proof.

If
N<2m
(resp.
N<m
), then a tuple in
\mathcal{I}^{m}_{0}
(resp.
\mathcal{I}^{m}_{1}
) contains at most
N
different elements and the proof proceeds similarly by truncating the sums over
p
.
∎

We now give more explicit computations for the case
m=2
. The sets
\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}
and
\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}
are detailed in Example
A.1
.

Proposition A.7
.

For any
h\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{2(t+1)}})
and
(k^{1}_{0:t-1},\cdots,k^{4}_{0:t-1})\in[N]^{4t}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in[N]^{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\neq k^{4}_{t}}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N(N-1)(N-2)(N-3)}{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}(g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h])^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1})

\displaystyle+\frac{N(N-1)(N-2)G_{\infty}^{3}|h|^{2}_{\infty}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\vartheta^{N}_{t}(\xi^{k^{1:4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})+\frac{N(N-1)G_{\infty}^{2}|h|^{2}_{\infty}}{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\upsilon^{N}_{t}(\xi^{k^{1:4}_{t-1}}_{t-1}),

and

\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in[N]^{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{2}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}=k^{4}_{t}}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\\
\leq\frac{N(N-1)}{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}(g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})+\frac{NG_{\infty}^{3}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1}).

where

\displaystyle\vartheta^{N}_{t}:x^{1:4}

\displaystyle\mapsto\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,x^{4})](x^{1})+\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,x^{4})](x^{2})+\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,x^{3})](x^{1})+\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,x^{3})](x^{2}),

\displaystyle\upsilon^{N}_{t}:x^{1:4}

\displaystyle\mapsto\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,x^{3})](x^{1})\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,x^{4})](x^{2})+\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,x^{4})](x^{1})\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,x^{3})](x^{2}).

Proof.

Let
(k^{1}_{0:t-1},\cdots,k^{4}_{0:t-1})\in[N]^{4t}
. First note that

\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in[N]^{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\neq k^{4}_{t}}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\\
=\sum_{p=2}^{4}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right].

We compute each term herebelow. For each
p\in[2:4]
and
\bm{k}:=(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}
, let
\bm{k}_{p}:=\{a_{1},\cdots,a_{p}\}=\{k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t}\}
the set of cardinal
p
containing the
p
distinct elements in a tuple
\bm{k}\in\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}
. Define for any
a_{i}\in\bm{k}_{p}
,
V_{a_{i}}:=\{k^{j}_{t}:j\in[1:4],\ k^{j}_{t}=a_{i}\}
.

-
Let
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}
. Then,
\bm{k}_{2}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t}\}
and we either have
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
and
V_{k^{2}}=\{k^{2}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
or
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1},k^{4}\}
and
V_{k^{2}_{t}}=\{k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
. Assume that
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
and
V_{k^{2}_{t}}=\{k^{2}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
. Then, by (
4.11
) in Lemma
4.1

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}

\displaystyle\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{3}_{t-1})\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]\mathbb{E}\bigg[\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{2}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{4}_{t-1})\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\int\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{3}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t})\int\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{4}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}}{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}).

If
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
and
V_{k^{2}_{t}}=\{k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}}{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\leq\frac{N(N-1)G_{\infty}^{2}}{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\bigg\{\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\bigg\}.

-
Let
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}
. Then, either
\bm{k}_{3}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
or
\bm{k}_{3}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
. Assume that
\bm{k}_{3}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
and
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
. Then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{W}^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathcal{W}^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{3}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1}).

Applying the same reasoning to all the combinations within
\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}
we get

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{N(N-1)(N-2)G_{\infty}^{3}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\bigg\{\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})+\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})+\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\bigg\}.

-
Let
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{4}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}
. Then,
