\bm{k}_{4}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\int h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})\phi_{t-1}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{j}_{t}}_{t})

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\int h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{4}\mathcal{W}^{k^{j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}(\xi^{k^{j}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\mathrm{d}\xi^{k^{j}_{t}}_{t}).

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=(g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h])^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1})/\Omega^{4}_{t-1},

which completes the proof of the first inequality. For the second inequality, write

\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in[N]^{4}}\mathbb{E}\left[h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{2}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}=k^{4}_{t}}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\\
=\sum_{p=1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{2}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right],

-
Let
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}
. Then
\bm{k}_{1}=\{k^{1}_{t}\}
and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{W}^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathcal{W}^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{3}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1}),

and

\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\prod_{j=1}^{2}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\leq\frac{NG_{\infty}^{3}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1}).

-
Let
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}
. Then,
\bm{k}_{2}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
,
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t}\}
and
V_{k^{3}_{t}}=\{k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
. Hence,

\mathbb{E}\left[h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{2}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\\
=(g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1})/\Omega^{4}_{t-1},

and

\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\prod_{j=1}^{2}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2j-1}_{t},k^{2j-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2j}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\right]\\
=\frac{N(N-1)}{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}(g_{t-1}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t-1}}_{0:t-1}),

which completes the proof.
∎

Proposition A.8
.

For any
t\in\mathbb{N}
,
h\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{2(t+1)}})
and
b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}
,

(i)

If
b_{t}=0
,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big\|^{2}_{2}\leq\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h])\big\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{N-2}{N-1}G_{\infty}^{3}|h|^{2}_{\infty}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\Omega_{t-1}}{N}\nu(\Theta_{b,t}^{N})\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 184.9429pt+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}|h|^{2}_{\infty}\Omega_{t-1}^{2}}{N(N-1)}\nu^{\otimes 2}(\Upsilon_{b,t}^{N})\bigg],

where

\displaystyle\Theta^{N}_{b,t}

\displaystyle:x\mapsto\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\big)+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(\mathbf{1}\otimes m_{t}(.,x)\big)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times\big[\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\big)\big],

\displaystyle\Upsilon^{N}_{b,t}

\displaystyle:(x,y)\mapsto\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y)\big)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y)\big)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\big(\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\big).

(ii)

If
b_{t}=1
,

\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big\|^{2}_{2}\leq\frac{N-1}{N}\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])\big\|^{2}_{2}\\
+G_{\infty}^{3}|h|_{\infty}^{2}\int\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\Omega_{t-1}}{N}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})\right]\nu(\mathrm{d}x).

(S1.29)

Proof.

To prove
(i)
, if
b_{t}=0
, by (
S1.2
),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0:t}\in[N]^{4(t+1)}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{1}_{0:t},k^{2}_{0:t})\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{3}_{0:t},k^{4}_{0:t})h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{4t}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t}(k^{3}_{0:t-1},k^{4}_{0:t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\bigg\{\sum_{p=2}^{4}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\frac{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{j}_{t},k^{j}_{t-1})h^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{0:t}}_{0:t},\cdots,\xi^{k^{4}_{0:t}}_{0:t})\bigg\},

and by Proposition
A.7
,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}\left\|\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h])\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 5.69046pt+\frac{G_{\infty}^{3}|h|^{2}_{\infty}(N-2)}{N(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\Omega_{t-1}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{4t}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{3}_{0:t-1},k^{4}_{0:t-1})\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\times\bigg\{\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})+\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\left.+\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})+\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\bigg\}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}|h|_{\infty}^{2}}{N(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[\Omega^{2}_{t-1}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{4t}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{3}_{0:t-1},k^{4}_{0:t-1})\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\lx@nobreakspace\times\bigg\{\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\left.+\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}\left[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\right](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\bigg\}\right].

Then using
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:A})}}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{4t}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{3}_{0:t-1},k^{4}_{0:t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})

\displaystyle=\int\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{4t}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{3}_{0:t-1},k^{4}_{0:t-1})\beta^{N}_{t}(x,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})m_{t}(\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1},x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)

\displaystyle=\int\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\right)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1}\right)\nu(\mathrm{d}x),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0:t-1}\in[N]^{4t}}\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{1}_{0:t-1},k^{2}_{0:t-1})\overline{\Lambda}^{1,2}_{b,t-1}(k^{3}_{0:t-1},k^{4}_{0:t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\int\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y))\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t-1}\left(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\right)\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}x,\mathrm{d}y).

which completes the proof of
(i)
by applying the same reasoning to the remaining terms. Item
(ii)
is obtained in the same way.
∎

A.10.
Supporting results for Theorem
4.9

In this section, we prove the analogues of Propositions
4.3
-
A.4
-
A.6
and
A.7
for
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}
. We remind the reader that the number of sampled indices
M
in the
PaRIS
estimator is
fixed
and that
\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t}
is defined in (
S1.15
). Let
\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}
be the following
\sigma
-algebra:

\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}:=\sigma\big(\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\cup\sigma(\xi^{1:N}_{t})\big).

(S1.30)

Lemma A.9
.

For any
t\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
,
h\in\mathbb{F}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{2}})
and
b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}
,

(i)

\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{b_{t}}_{t}[h])
,

(ii)

\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)
is an unbiased estimator of
\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}(h)
,

where
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)
is defined in (
4.31
).

Proof.

We start with the case
b_{t}=0
. For any
(k,\ell)\in[N]^{2}
,
J^{i}_{k,t-1}
and
J^{i}_{\ell,t-1}
are independent conditionally on
\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}
for any
i\in[M]
if
k\neq\ell
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}
is
\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}
-measurable, hence, using (
4.4
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k,\ell)h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t})\middle|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

(S1.31)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t})M^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{i}_{k,t-1},J^{i}_{\ell,t-1})\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t})M^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{n,m\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k,n)\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(\ell,m)\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(n,m)\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\sum_{n,m\in[N]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(n,m)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k,n)\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(\ell,m)h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t})\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}}{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\sum_{n,m\in[N]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(n,m)\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h]\big)(\xi^{n}_{t-1},\xi^{m}_{t-1}).

If
b_{t}=1
,

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k,\ell)h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t})\middle|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\right]

(S1.32)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbbm{1}_{k=\ell}h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t})M^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{m=1}^{N}\mathcal{W}^{m}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{i}_{k,t-1},m)\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}_{k=\ell}h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t})M^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{n,m\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k,n)\mathcal{W}^{m}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(n,m)\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\sum_{n,m\in[N]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(n,m)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}_{k=\ell}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k,n)\mathcal{W}^{m}_{t-1}h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t})\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{k=\ell}}{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\sum_{n,m\in[N]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(n,m)\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h]\big)(\xi^{n}_{t-1},\xi^{m}_{t-1}).

Consequently, if
b_{t}=0
, by (
S1.31
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\prod_{s=0}^{t-1}N^{b_{s}}\left(\frac{N}{N-1}\right)^{1-b_{s}}\frac{\gamma_{t-1}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}{N^{2}}\sum_{k,\ell\in[N]^{2}}\frac{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}{N(N-1)}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k,\ell)h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t})\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\prod_{s=0}^{t-1}N^{b_{s}}\left(\frac{N}{N-1}\right)^{1-b_{s}}\frac{\gamma_{t-1}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}{N^{2}}\sum_{n,m\in[N]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(n,m)\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h]\big)(\xi^{n}_{t-1},\xi^{m}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h]),

and in a similar way,
\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(h)\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])
by (
S1.32
) if
b_{t}=1
. The second item follows straightforwardly by induction and the tower property. The induction is initialized by noting that
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,0}(h)
is equal to
\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,0}(h)
which is an unbiased estimator of
\mathcal{Q}_{b,0}(h)
by Proposition
4.3
.
∎

Proposition A.10
.

Let
t>0
and
N\geq 4
.

(i)

If
b_{t}=0
,

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}}h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]\\
=\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h])^{2},

(S1.33)

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}(M-1)\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}{MN(N-1)}\nu^{\otimes 2}(\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{b,t}^{N,M})\\
+\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}{MN(N-1)}\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\mathsf{T}^{(1)}_{t-1,b}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1}),

(S1.34)

and

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]\leq\frac{\Omega_{t-1}G_{\infty}^{3}(M-1)(N-2)}{MN(N-1)}\nu(\widetilde{\Theta}_{b,t}^{N,M})\\
+\frac{\Omega_{t-1}G_{\infty}^{3}(N-2)}{MN(N-1)}\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:3}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\mathsf{T}^{(2)}_{t-1,b}(k^{1:3}_{t-1}),

(S1.35)

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Theta}^{N,M}_{b,t}

\displaystyle:x\mapsto\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1}\big)+\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(\mathbf{1}\otimes m_{t}(.,x)\big)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})+\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\big)\big],

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{N,M}_{b,t}

\displaystyle:(x,y)\mapsto\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y)\big)\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y)\big)\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\big),

and

\displaystyle\mathsf{T}^{(1)}_{b,t}

\displaystyle:(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\mapsto\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})^{2}+\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{1}_{t}),

\displaystyle\mathsf{T}^{(2)}_{b,t}

\displaystyle:(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t})\mapsto\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{1}_{t})+\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{3}_{t})

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t})+\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{2}_{t}).

(ii)

If
b_{t}=1
,

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]\\
=\frac{N-1}{N}\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h])^{2},

(S1.36)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

(S1.37)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{\Omega_{t-1}G_{\infty}^{3}}{N}\int\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes 1)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\frac{G_{\infty}^{3}\Omega_{t-1}}{MN}\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1,2,4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1}).

Proof.

We start with the case
b_{t}=0
.

-
If
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}
, then
k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}\neq k^{3}_{t}\neq k^{4}_{t}
and conditionally on
\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}
,
J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1}
,
J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1}
,
J^{j}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1}
and
J^{j}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1}
are independent for any
(i,j)\in[M]^{2}
and
J^{1:M}_{k^{\ell}_{t},t-1}\overset{\mathrm{iid}}{\sim}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{\ell}_{t},.)
for any
\ell\in[1:4]
, thus, for any
(i,j)\in[M]^{2}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1}

\displaystyle,J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{j}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},J^{j}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{i}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},J^{i}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\prod_{n=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1}),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{j}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},J^{j}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\prod_{n=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt\times\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h]\big)^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1}),

where we have used that
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})
are
\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}
-measurable in the third equality and then proceeded similarly to Proposition
A.7
. By Example
A.1
,
\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2})=N(N-1)(N-2)(N-3)
and since
b_{t}=0
implies that

\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}=\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\frac{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}},

(S1.38)

we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}}h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{2}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{j}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},J^{j}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{(N-2)(N-3)}{N(N-1)}\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{0}_{t}[h]\big)^{2}.

-
If
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}
. Then
\bm{k}_{2}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t}\}
and we either have
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
and
V_{k^{2}_{t}}=\{k^{2}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
or
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
and
V_{k^{2}_{t}}=\{k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
. Assume that
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
and
V_{k^{2}_{t}}=\{k^{2}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
. Taking into account that
J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1}=J^{i}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1}
and
J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1}=J^{i}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip-113.81102pt=\mathbb{E}\bigg[M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{j}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},J^{j}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1})\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-113.81102pt=\mathbb{E}\bigg[M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip-99.58464pt+M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{\ell}_{t},k^{\ell}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg].

Then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}}{M\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})^{2}+\frac{(M-1)G_{\infty}^{2}}{M\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1}).

Similarly, if
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
and
V_{k^{2}_{t}}=\{k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}}{M\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2}_{t-1},k^{1}_{t-1})+\frac{(M-1)G_{\infty}^{2}}{M\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})](\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1}).

Consequently,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}N(N-1)}{M\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\left\{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})^{2}+\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2}_{t-1},k^{1}_{t-1})\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}N(N-1)(M-1)}{M\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\int\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\bigg\{m_{t}(\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1},x)\beta^{N}_{t}(x,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})m_{t}(\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1},y)\beta^{N}_{t}(y,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})+

\displaystyle m_{t}(\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1},x)\beta^{N}_{t}(x,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})m_{t}(\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1},y)\beta^{N}_{t}(y,\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\bigg\}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}x,\mathrm{d}y).

Then, using (
S1.38
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}{MN(N-1)}\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\left\{\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})^{2}+\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2}_{t-1},k^{1}_{t-1})\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{G_{\infty}^{2}(M-1)\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}{MN(N-1)}\int\bigg\{\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y)\big)\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y)\big)\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(\beta^{N}_{t}(y,.)\otimes\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\big)\bigg\}\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}x,\mathrm{d}y),

which yields (
S1.34
).

-
If
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}
. Then either
\bm{k}_{3}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
or
\bm{k}_{3}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
. Assume that
\bm{k}_{3}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t},k^{3}_{t}\}
. Then
V_{k^{1}_{t}}=\{k^{1}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\}
,
J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1}=J^{i}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1}
for any
i\in[M]
and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},J^{i}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{j}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},J^{j}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1})\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\sum_{k^{1:3}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\prod_{i=1}^{3}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{i}_{t},k^{i}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{1}_{t-1})

\displaystyle+M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{\ell}_{t},k^{\ell}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{3}}{M\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\sum_{k^{1:3}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{1}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{(M-1)G_{\infty}^{3}}{M\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\mathrm{M}_{t}[\beta^{N}_{t}(.,\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1})](\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1}).

The remaining combinations are treated in the exact same way and (
S1.35
) is obtained by using again (
S1.38
).

Consider now the case
b_{t}=1
and let
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}
. Then
k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}
,
k^{3}_{t}=k^{4}_{t}
and
k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{3}_{t}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\sum_{k^{2,4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathcal{W}^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\times\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{j}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t},k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2\ell}_{t-1}}_{t-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\times\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h]\big)^{\otimes 2}(\xi^{k^{1}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\xi^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\xi^{k^{3}_{t-1}}_{t-1},\xi^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1}).

Since
b_{t}=1
implies that

\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}=\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\frac{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}{N^{2}},

and using
\mathrm{Card}(\mathcal{I}^{1}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2})=N(N-1)
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[h(\xi^{k^{1}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{2}_{t}}_{t})h(\xi^{k^{3}_{t}}_{t},\xi^{k^{4}_{t}}_{t})M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\sum_{k^{2,4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathcal{W}^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\times\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(J^{j}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{N-1}{N}\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}\big(g^{\otimes 2}_{t-1}\mathcal{M}^{1}_{t}[h]\big)^{2},

which proves (
S1.36
).

Let
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{4}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}
. Then
\bm{k}_{1}=\{k^{1}_{t}\}
,
k^{1}_{t}=k^{2}_{t}=k^{3}_{t}=k^{4}_{t}
and
J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1}=J^{i}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[M^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[M]^{2}}\sum_{k^{2,4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathcal{W}^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{i}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(J^{j}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{1}{M}\sum_{k^{1,2,4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\mathcal{W}^{k^{4}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})

\displaystyle+\frac{M-1}{M}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t},k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1})\mathcal{W}^{k^{2\ell}_{t-1}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{G_{\infty}^{3}}{M\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\sum_{k^{1,2,4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\frac{G_{\infty}^{3}(M-1)}{M\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\int\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})\nu(\mathrm{d}x).

This yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{3}\Omega_{t-1}}{MN}\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1,2,4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\frac{G_{\infty}^{3}(M-1)\Omega_{t-1}}{MN}\int\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes\mathbf{1})\nu(\mathrm{d}x),

which in turn proves (
S1.37
).
∎

Proposition A.11
.

Let
(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B}:\ref *{assp:boundup}})
hold. Let
t\geq 0
. For any
b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}

(i)

If
b_{t}=0
, then for
p\in\{2,3\}
,

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]=0.

(S1.39)

(ii)

If
b_{t}=1
, then

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]=0.

(S1.40)

Additionally, if
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:boundbelow})}}
holds then the rate of convergence is
\mathcal{O}(N^{-1})
.

Proof.

We prove
(i)
-
(ii)
simultaneously by induction. Let
t=0
and
b\in\mathcal{B}_{0}
. By definition,
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{0}=\mathcal{T}^{b}_{0}
and,
if
b_{0}=0
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{N,b,0}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{0}(k^{1}_{0},k^{2}_{0})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{0}(k^{3}_{0},k^{4}_{0})=\frac{2}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}=\mathcal{O}(N^{-2}),

(S1.41)

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{N,b,0}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{0}(k^{1}_{0},k^{2}_{0})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{0}(k^{3}_{0},k^{4}_{0})=\frac{4}{N^{2}(N-1)^{2}}\sum_{i,j,k\in[N]^{3}}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq k}=\mathcal{O}(N^{-1}).

(S1.42)

If
b_{0}=1
, then

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{N,b,0}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{0}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{0}(k^{1}_{0},k^{2}_{0})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{0},k^{4}_{0})=\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}=\mathcal{O}(N^{-1}).

(S1.43)

Let
t>0
and assume that both
(i)
-
(ii)
hold at
t-1
. Define

\displaystyle D^{N}_{1,b}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1,2,4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\bigg],

\displaystyle D^{N}_{2,b}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\mathsf{T}^{(1)}_{t-1,b}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\bigg],

\displaystyle D^{N}_{3,b}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:3}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\mathsf{T}^{(2)}_{t-1,b}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1},k^{3}_{t-1})\bigg],

where
\mathsf{T}^{(1)}_{t-1,b}
and
\mathsf{T}^{(2)}_{t-1,b}
are defined in Proposition
A.10
.
If
b_{t}=0
, then by
(i)
in Proposition
A.10
, using that
\Omega_{t-1}\leq NG_{\infty}
,

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]\\
\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}G_{\infty}^{2}(M-1)}{MN(N-1)}\int\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{b,t}^{N,M}(x,y)\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}x,\mathrm{d}y)\bigg]+2\frac{G_{\infty}^{4}}{M}D_{2,b}^{N},

(S1.44)

and

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]\\
\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\Omega_{t-1}G_{\infty}^{3}(M-1)(N-2)}{MN(N-1)}\int\widetilde{\Theta}_{b,t}^{N,M}(x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)\bigg]+\frac{G_{\infty}^{4}}{M}D^{N}_{3,b},

(S1.45)

where
\widetilde{\Theta}_{b,t}^{N,M}
and
\widetilde{\Upsilon}_{b,t}^{N,M}
are defined in Proposition
A.10
.
We deal first with
D^{N}_{2,b}
and
D^{N}_{3,b}
. If
b_{t-1}=0
, then by definition of
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}
in (
4.4
),

\displaystyle D^{N}_{2,b}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\bigg],

(S1.46)

\displaystyle D^{N}_{3,b}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\bigg].

If
b_{t-1}=1
,

\displaystyle D^{N}_{2,b}

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\bigg],

(S1.47)

\displaystyle D^{N}_{3,b}

\displaystyle=4\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1})\bigg].

In all cases, by the induction hypothesis we get for any
b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}
with
b_{t}=0
,

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}D^{N}_{2,b}=0,\quad\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}D^{N}_{3,b}=0.

Regarding the first terms in the r.h.s. of inequalities (
S1.44
)-(
S1.45
), they go to zero when
N
goes to infinity since they are, up to the constant
(M-1)/M\leq 1
, the
PaRIS
counterpart of
B_{N}
(
S1.12
) in the proof of Theorem
4.4
and are treated in the exact same way since
\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbb{E}\big[\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})^{3}\big]<\infty
by Proposition
A.12
.
If
b_{t}=1
, then, by Proposition
A.10
, using that
\Omega_{t-1}\leq NG_{\infty}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{1}\cap\mathcal{S}^{1}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\Omega_{t-1}G_{\infty}^{3}}{N}\int\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(m_{t}.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\beta^{N}_{t}(x,.)\otimes 1)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)\bigg]+\frac{G_{\infty}^{4}}{M}D^{N}_{1,b}.

The first term goes to zero when
N
goes to infinity similarly to the case
b_{t}=0
. As for the second term, if
b_{t-1}=0
then by definition of
D^{N}_{1,b}

0\leq D^{N}_{1,b}\leq D^{N}_{3,b},

and if
b_{t-1}=1
,

0\leq D^{N}_{1,b}=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})^{2}\bigg]\leq D^{N}_{2,b}.

Hence, in both cases
D^{N}_{1,b}
goes to zero by (
A.10
). This ends the proof of the first claim.

Now assume that
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:boundbelow})}}
holds also. We proceed by induction. At
t=0
the rate of convergence is
\mathcal{O}(N^{-1})
by (
S1.41
)-(
S1.42
) and (
S1.43
). Let
t>0
and assume that the rate of convergence in
(i)
and
(ii)
at
t-1
is
\mathcal{O}(N^{-1})
. Assume that
b_{t}=0
. By the strong mixing assumption we have that

\beta^{N}_{t}(x,y)\leq\frac{G_{\infty}\sigma_{+}}{\sigma_{-}\Omega_{t-1}},\quad\forall(x,y)\in\mathsf{X}^{2}.

(S1.48)

Using for example that

\int\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes\mathbf{1})\nu(\mathrm{d}x)=\int\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(m_{t}(.,x)\otimes m_{t}(.,y))\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}x,\mathrm{d}y)=\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1}),

and then bounding
\beta^{N}_{t}
using (
S1.48
) we get that

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}G_{\infty}^{2}(M-1)}{MN(N-1)}\int\widetilde{\Upsilon}^{N,M}_{b,t}(x,y)\nu^{\otimes 2}(\mathrm{d}x,\mathrm{d}y)\bigg]\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{4}\sigma_{+}^{2}(M-1)}{\sigma_{-}^{2}MN(N-1)}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})\big\|^{2}_{2},

(S1.49)

and

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\Omega_{t-1}G_{\infty}^{3}(M-1)(N-2)}{MN(N-1)}\int\widetilde{\Theta}_{b,t}^{N,M}(x)\nu(\mathrm{d}x)\bigg]\leq\frac{4\sigma_{+}G_{\infty}^{4}(M-1)(N-2)}{\sigma_{-}MN(N-1)}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})\big\|_{2}^{2},

where both bounds are
\mathcal{O}(N^{-1})
by Proposition
A.12
. Going back to (
S1.44
)-(
S1.45
), we obtain

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{2}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]\leq\frac{2G_{\infty}^{4}\sigma_{+}^{2}(M-1)}{\sigma_{-}^{2}MN(N-1)}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})\big\|^{2}_{2}+2\frac{G_{\infty}^{4}}{M}D^{N}_{2,b},

and

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{2}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{3}_{2}}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{2}_{t})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t})\bigg]\\
\leq\frac{4\sigma_{+}G_{\infty}^{4}(M-1)(N-2)}{\sigma_{-}MN(N-1)}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1}\otimes\mathbf{1})\big\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{G_{\infty}^{4}}{M}D^{N}_{3,b}.

By (
S1.46
)-(
S1.47
) and the induction hypothesis, we get that
D^{N}_{2,b}
and
D^{N}_{3,b}
are both
\mathcal{O}(N^{-1})
. Finally, applying Proposition
A.12
we get
\mathcal{O}(N^{-1})
upper bounds. This ends the proof for the case
b_{t}=0
. The case
b_{t}=1
follows the same steps.
∎

Proposition A.12
.

Assume that
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B})}}
holds. For all
M>1
,
t\in\mathbb{N}
and
b\in\mathcal{B}_{t}
,

\sup_{N\in\mathbb{N}}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})\big\|_{3}<\infty.

(S1.50)

Proof.

We proceed by induction on
t\in\mathbb{N}
. Assume for now that
N\geq 6
and
M\geq 2
.

Since
\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,0}(h)=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,0}(h)
for any
h
, the case
t=0
follows from Proposition
A.4
. Let
t>0
and assume that (
S1.50
) holds at
t-1
. We only treat the case
b_{t}=0
. The proof for the case
b_{t}=1
follows using the same steps. Since we have that

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})\big\|^{3}_{3}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t},k^{2\ell}_{t})\bigg]

(S1.51)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}}{M^{3}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}}\sum_{i^{1:3}\in[M]^{3}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg],

the proof proceeds by (i) splitting the sum in three parts with respect to the cardinal of the triplet
(i^{1},i^{2},i^{3})\in[M]^{3}
, and (ii) bounding each term by
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})\|^{3}_{3}
up to a constant independent of
N
.
Let
(i^{1},i^{2},i^{3})\in[M]^{3}
. If
\mathrm{Card}(\{i^{1},i^{2},i^{3}\})=3
, then for all
k^{1:6}_{t}\in[N]^{6}
,
(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1})_{\ell\in[1:3]}
are mutually independent conditionally on
\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}
, defined in (
S1.30
), and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\sum_{k^{2\ell-1:2\ell}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t},k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2\ell}_{t},k^{2\ell}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1}\big)\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{6}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})\bigg|\lx@nobreakspace\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg].

Hence, by Proposition
A.6
, Lemma
A.5
and similarly to the proof of Proposition
A.4
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1}\big)\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{6}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})\bigg|\lx@nobreakspace\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1}\big)\sum_{p=2}^{6}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{3}}\frac{G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\sum_{p=2}^{6}\frac{N^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}}\bigg)\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1}\big),

where
\lesssim
means less than or equal up to a multiplicative constant independent of
N
. Consequently,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}}{M^{3}}

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}}\sum_{i^{1:3}\in[M]^{3}}\mathbbm{1}_{i^{1}\neq i^{2}\neq i^{3}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\frac{\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}}{M^{3}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}}\sum_{i^{1:3}\in[M]^{3}}\mathbbm{1}_{i^{1}\neq i^{2}\neq i^{3}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\frac{M(M-1)(M-2)}{M^{3}}\bigg(\sum_{p=2}^{6}\frac{N^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}}\bigg)\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1}\big),

and

\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}\bigg(\sum_{p=2}^{6}\frac{N^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}}\bigg)=\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{3}\bigg(\sum_{p=2}^{6}\frac{N^{p}\Omega^{6}_{t-1}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}N^{3}(N-1)^{3}}\bigg)\\
\leq\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{3}\lx@nobreakspace\bigg(\sum_{p=2}^{6}\frac{G_{\infty}^{6-p}N^{6}}{N^{3}(N-1)^{3}}\bigg)\lesssim\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t-1}^{3}.

Therefore,

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}}{M^{3}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in[N]^{6}}\sum_{i^{1:3}\in[M]^{3}}\mathbbm{1}_{i^{1}\neq i^{2}\neq i^{3}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg]\lesssim\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})\big\|_{3}^{3}.

(S1.52)

Assume now that
\mathrm{Card}(\{i^{1},i^{2},i^{3}\})=2
and that
i^{1}=i^{2}
. For all
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{6}_{t})\in[N]^{6}
, conditionally on
\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{3}}_{k^{5}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{3}}_{k^{6}_{t},t-1}\big)
is independent from
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{1}}_{k^{1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{1}}_{k^{2}_{t},t-1}\big)
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{2}}_{k^{3}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{2}}_{k^{4}_{t},t-1}\big)
, hence, using (
S1.5
),

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]

(S1.53)

\displaystyle=\bigg\{\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\lx@nobreakspace\hskip 56.9055pt\times\bigg\{\sum_{k^{5:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{1}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{3}}_{k^{5}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{3}}_{k^{6}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle=\big(F^{N}_{2,b}+F^{N}_{3,b}+F^{N}_{4,b}\big)F^{N}_{1,b},

with

\displaystyle F^{N}_{1,b}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k^{5:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{1}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{3}}_{k^{5}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{3}}_{k^{6}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{5:6}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{5}_{t}\neq k^{6}_{t}}\sum_{k^{5:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{5}_{t},k^{5}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{6}_{t},k^{6}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{5}_{t-1},k^{6}_{t-1}),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt=\sum_{p=2}^{4}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{2}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\vee\xi^{1:N}_{t}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\lx@nobreakspace=F^{N}_{2,b}+F^{N}_{3,b}+F^{N}_{4,b},

where

\displaystyle F^{N}_{2,b}:=\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t}\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{2}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}}\sum_{k^{1:2}_{t-1}\in[N]^{2}}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{1}_{t},k^{1}_{t-1})\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{2}_{t},k^{2}_{t-1})\mathsf{T}^{(1)}_{t-1,b}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})

\displaystyle F^{N}_{3,b}:=\sum_{k^{1:3}_{t}\in[N]^{3}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}\neq k^{3}_{t}}\sum_{k^{1:3}_{t-1}\in[N]^{3}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{\ell}_{t},k^{\ell}_{t-1})\mathsf{T}^{(2)}_{t-1,b}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1},k^{3}_{t-1})

\displaystyle F^{N}_{4,b}:=\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t}\in[N]^{4}}\mathbbm{1}_{k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}\neq k^{3}_{t}\neq k^{4}_{t}}\sum_{k^{1:4}_{t-1}\in[N]^{4}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{4}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{\ell}_{t},k^{\ell}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{3}_{t-1},k^{4}_{t-1}),

where
\mathsf{T}^{(1)}_{t-1,b}
and
\mathsf{T}^{(2)}_{t-1,b}
are defined in Proposition
A.10
.
We now upperbound each
\mathbb{E}[F^{N}_{1,b}F^{N}_{i,b}|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}]
for
i\in[2:4]
.

Consider first the case
\mathbb{E}[F^{N}_{1,b}F^{N}_{4,b}|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}]
. Let
S_{6}:=\{k^{1:6}\in[N]^{6}:k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}\neq k^{3}_{t}\neq k^{4}_{t},k^{5}_{t}\neq k^{6}_{t}\}.
Then,
S_{6}\subset\big(\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{4}_{3}\big)\sqcup\big(\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{5}_{3}\big)\sqcup\big(\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{6}_{3}\big)
and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[F^{N}_{1,b}F^{N}_{4,b}\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1})\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathsf{S}_{6}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{6}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1})\sum_{p=4}^{6}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{3}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{6}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\sum_{p=4}^{6}\frac{N^{p}G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}}\bigg)\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1}),

(S1.54)

by Proposition
A.6
and Lemma
A.5
.

Consider now the case
\mathbb{E}[F^{N}_{1,b}F^{N}_{3,b}|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}]
and define

\mathsf{S}_{5}:=\{k^{1:5}_{t}\in[N]^{5}:k^{1}_{t}\neq k^{2}_{t}\neq k^{3}_{t},k^{4}_{t}\neq k^{5}_{t}\}
. Then,

\mathbb{E}\big[F^{N}_{1,b}F^{N}_{3,b}\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]\\
=\sum_{k^{1:5}_{t-1}\in[N]^{5}}\mathsf{T}^{(2)}_{t-1,b}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1},k^{3}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{4}_{t-1},k^{5}_{t-1})\sum_{k^{1:5}_{t}\in\mathsf{S}_{5}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{5}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg].

Proceeding similarly as in Proposition
A.6
and Lemma
A.5
, it can be shown that

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:5}_{t}\in\mathsf{S}_{5}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{5}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]\leq\frac{N^{5}G_{\infty}^{5}}{\Omega^{5}_{t-1}}+\frac{2N^{4}G_{\infty}^{4}}{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}+\frac{N^{3}G_{\infty}^{3}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}.

Finally, by noting that

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:5}_{t-1}\in[N]^{5}}\mathsf{T}^{(2)}_{t-1,b}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1},k^{3}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{4}_{t-1},k^{5}_{t-1})\leq 4\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1})

we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[

\displaystyle F^{N}_{1,b}F^{N}_{3,b}\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{N^{5}G_{\infty}^{5}}{\Omega^{5}_{t-1}}+\frac{2N^{4}G_{\infty}^{4}}{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}+\frac{N^{3}G_{\infty}^{3}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\bigg)\sum_{k^{1:5}_{t-1}\in[N]^{5}}\mathsf{T}^{(2)}_{t-1,b}(k^{1}_{t-1},k^{2}_{t-1},k^{3}_{t-1})\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{4}_{t-1},k^{5}_{t-1})

\displaystyle\leq 4\bigg(\frac{N^{5}G_{\infty}^{5}}{\Omega^{5}_{t-1}}+\frac{2N^{4}G_{\infty}^{4}}{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}+\frac{N^{3}G_{\infty}^{3}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}\bigg)\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1}).

(S1.55)

Consider now the case
\mathbb{E}[F^{N}_{1,b}F^{N}_{2,b}|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}]
. In the same way as for the previous case, we obtain

\mathbb{E}\big[F^{N}_{1,b}F^{N}_{2,b}\big|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\big]\leq 2\left(\frac{N^{4}G_{\infty}^{4}}{\Omega^{4}_{t-1}}+\frac{2N^{3}G_{\infty}^{3}}{\Omega^{3}_{t-1}}+\frac{N^{2}G_{\infty}^{2}}{\Omega^{2}_{t-1}}\right)\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t-1}).

(S1.56)

Thus, combining (
S1.56
), (
A.10
) and (
A.10
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in[N]^{6}}\sum_{i^{1:3}\in[M]^{3}}\mathbbm{1}_{i^{1}=i^{2},i^{3}\notin\{i^{1},i^{2}\}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg(\sum_{p=2}^{6}\frac{\Omega^{6}_{t-1}N^{p}G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}N^{3}(N-1)^{3}}\bigg)\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})^{3}\bigg]\lesssim\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})\big\|^{3}_{3}.

The other triplets
(i^{1},i^{2},i^{3})
for which
\mathrm{Card}(\{i^{1},i^{2},i^{3}\})=2
are handled similarly and are bounded by
\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})\big\|^{3}_{3}
up to some multiplicative constant independent of
N
. We finally obtain

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}}{M^{3}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in[N]^{6}}\sum_{i^{1:3}\in[M]^{3}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathrm{Card}(\{i^{1},i^{2},i^{3}\})=2}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg]\lesssim\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})\big\|^{3}_{3}.

(S1.57)

It now remains to treat the case
\mathrm{Card}(\{i^{1},i^{2},i^{3}\})=1
. Let
(k^{1},\cdots,k^{d})\in[N]^{d}
. Denote by
\mathsf{Pos}_{d}(k^{1:d})
the set of elements in
[N]^{d}
with positions of equal elements similar to those of the equal elements in
k^{1:d}
. For example,

\displaystyle\mathsf{Pos}_{3}((2,1,1))

\displaystyle=\{(j,i,i)\mid(i,j)\in[N]^{2},i\neq j\},

\displaystyle\mathsf{Pos}_{3}((1,1,2))

\displaystyle=\{(i,i,j)\mid(i,j)\in[N]^{2},i\neq j\},

\displaystyle\mathsf{Pos}_{3}((1,2,3))

\displaystyle=\{(i,j,k)\mid(i,j,k)\in[N]^{3},\mathrm{Card}(\{i,j,k\})=3\}.

Let
p\in[2:6]
and
(k^{1}_{t},\cdots,k^{6}_{t})\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{3}
. Without loss of generality, assume that the first
p
elements of
k^{1:6}_{t}
are all different.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},

\displaystyle J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in\mathsf{Pos}_{6}(k^{1:6}_{t})}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t})\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{p}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t}(k^{n}_{t},k^{n}_{t-1})\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega_{t-1}^{p}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in\mathsf{Pos}_{6}(k^{1:6}_{t})}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega_{t-1}^{p}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t}).

Consequently, by Lemma
A.5
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{3}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg|\mathcal{G}^{N}_{t-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in\mathcal{I}^{3}_{0}\cap\mathcal{S}^{p}_{3}}\frac{G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega_{t-1}^{p}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{N^{p}G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega^{p}_{t-1}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t-1}\in[N]^{6}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}(k^{2\ell-1}_{t-1},k^{2\ell}_{t}),

and,

\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}}{M^{3}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in[N]^{6}}\sum_{i^{1:3}\in[M]^{3}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathrm{Card}(\{i^{1},i^{2},i^{3}\})=1}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg]\\
\lesssim\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg(\sum_{p=2}^{6}\frac{\Omega_{t-1}^{6}N^{p}G_{\infty}^{p}}{\Omega_{t-1}^{p}N^{3}(N-1)^{3}}\bigg)\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})^{3}\bigg]\lesssim\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})\big\|^{3}_{3}.

(S1.58)

Finally, combining (
S1.52
), (
S1.57
) and (
S1.58
) we get

\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})\big\|^{3}_{3}=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{\mathcal{C}_{N,b,t}^{3}}{M^{3}}\sum_{k^{1:6}_{t}\in[N]^{6}}\sum_{i^{1:3}\in[M]^{3}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{b}_{t-1}\big(J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell-1}_{t},t-1},J^{i^{\ell}}_{k^{2\ell}_{t},t-1}\big)\bigg]\lesssim\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t-1}(\mathbf{1})\big\|^{3}_{3},

and hence
\sup_{N\geq 6}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{Q}}^{N,M}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})\big\|_{3}<\infty
by the induction hypothesis. This ends the proof for the case
b_{t}=0
.

If
M=2
, then (
S1.52
) is equal to
0
and (
S1.57
), (
S1.58
) remain the same. The result then follows. If
N<6
then it suffices to truncate the sums over
p
to obtain the result.
∎

Appendix B
Further algorithmic details

B.1.
Alternative expression of the genealogy tracing variance estimator

The expression of the CLE estimator (
3.11
) provided in the main paper is different from the expression of the estimator appearing in
[
32
]
. We show here that these are two expressions of the same quantity. Note first that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle=N^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathrm{E}^{i}_{t,0}=\mathrm{E}^{j}_{t,0}}\{h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}\{h(\xi^{j}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+N^{-2}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathrm{E}^{i}_{t,0}\neq\mathrm{E}^{j}_{t,0}}\{h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}\{h(\xi^{j}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathrm{E}^{i}_{t,0}=\mathrm{E}^{j}_{t,0}}

\displaystyle\{h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}\{h(\xi^{j}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{N}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathrm{E}^{i}_{t,0}=\mathrm{E}^{j}_{t,0}=k}\{h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}\{h(\xi^{j}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{N}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathrm{E}^{i}_{t,0}=k}\{h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}\right)^{2}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{N}_{\eta,t}(h)

\displaystyle=-N^{-1}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathrm{E}^{i}_{t,0}\neq\mathrm{E}^{j}_{t,0}}\{h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}\{h(\xi^{j}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{N}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathrm{E}^{i}_{t,0}=k}\{h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}\right)^{2}.

where the expression in the second line is that of
[
32
]
. By a similar reasoning, (
3.12
) is also equivalent to their estimator.

B.2.
Variance estimators for the predictor and filter

The asymptotic variances of the predictor and filter (
3.8
)-(
3.9
) can be expressed using
\mathcal{V}^{\infty}_{\gamma,t}
. Indeed,

\frac{\mathcal{V}^{\infty}_{\gamma,t}(h-\eta_{t}(h))}{\gamma_{t}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}=\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left\{\frac{\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s}\big(\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t}[h-\eta_{t}(h)]^{2}\big)}{\gamma_{t}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}-\eta_{t}(h-\eta_{t}(h))^{2}\right\}=\mathcal{V}^{\infty}_{\eta_{,}t}(h),

and using that

\frac{\gamma_{t}(g_{t}\{h-\phi_{t}(h)\})}{\gamma_{t+1}(\mathbf{1})}=\frac{\gamma_{t}(g_{t}h)}{\gamma_{t+1}(\mathbf{1})}-\phi_{t}(h)=0,

we get

\mathcal{V}^{\infty}_{\phi,t}(h)=\frac{\mathcal{V}^{\infty}_{\gamma,t}\big(g_{t}\{h-\phi_{t}(h)\}\big)}{\gamma_{t+1}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}.

(S2.1)

Then, replacing
\gamma_{t}(h)
and
\phi_{t}(h)

by their empirical approximations

\gamma_{t}^{N}(h)
and
\phi_{t}^{N}(h)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\eta,t}(h)

\displaystyle:=\frac{-N^{t}}{(N-1)^{t+1}}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathcal{T}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t}(i,j)\big\{h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\big\}\big\{h(\xi^{j}_{t})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\big\},

(S2.2)

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\phi,t}(h)

\displaystyle:=\frac{-N^{t+2}}{(N-1)^{t+1}}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathcal{W}^{i}_{t}\mathcal{W}^{j}_{t}\mathcal{T}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t}(i,j)\big\{h(\xi^{i}_{t})-\phi_{t}^{N}(h)\big\}\big\{h(\xi^{j}_{t})-\phi_{t}^{N}(h)\big\}.

(S2.3)

As a consequence of Theorem
4.7
, these estimators are also weakly consistent.

Corollary B.1
.

Let
(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{\ref *{assp:A}:\ref *{assp:boundup}})
hold. For any
h\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X})
,
\mathcal{V}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\eta,t}(h)\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathcal{V}^{\infty}_{\eta,t}(h)
and
\mathcal{V}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\phi,t}(h)\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathcal{V}^{\infty}_{\phi,t}(h)
.

Proof.

It suffices to note that
\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}
and
\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}
are bilinear, that
\eta^{N}_{t}(h)\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\eta_{t}(h)
and to apply Theorem
4.4
again:

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}\left(\{h-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\}^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h^{\otimes 2})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h\otimes\mathbf{1})-\eta_{t}^{N}(h)\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1}\otimes h)+\eta_{t}^{N}(h)^{2}\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathcal{Q}_{b,t}\left(\{h-\eta_{t}(h)\}^{\otimes 2}\right).

Hence,
\mathcal{V}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{\gamma,t}(h-\eta_{t}^{N}(h))\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\mathcal{V}^{\infty}_{\gamma,t}(h-\eta_{t}(h))
and using the fact that
\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\longrightarrow}\gamma_{t}(\mathbf{1})^{2}
we get the consistency for the predictive measures. The remaining limit is a straightforward application.
∎

Algorithm 3

Update at step
t+1
of the variance estimator for the predictor

\omega^{1:N}_{t},\xi^{1:N}_{t},\xi^{1:N}_{t+1}
and
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t}

Compute
\bm{\beta}^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t+1}

if

PaRIS

then

for

k\in[1:N]

do

Sample
J^{1:M}_{k,t}\overset{\mathrm{iid}}{\sim}\beta^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t+1}(k,.)

end

for

for

(k,\ell)\in[1:N]^{2}

do

Set
\mathcal{T}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t+1}(k,\ell)=\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\mathcal{T}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t}(J^{i}_{k,t},J^{i}_{\ell,t})/M

end

for

else

Compute
\overline{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t+1}=\bm{\beta}^{\mathsf{BS}}_{t+1}\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t}\bm{\beta}^{\mathsf{BS}\prime}_{t+1}
.

Set
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t+1}=\overline{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t+1}-\mathrm{Diag}(\overline{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t+1})
.

end

if

Compute
\bm{\mathcal{Q}}=\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t+1}\odot\big[\big\{h(\xi^{1:N}_{t+1})-\eta_{t+1}^{N}(h)\big\}\big\{h(\xi^{1:N}_{t+1})-\eta_{t+1}^{N}(h)\big\}^{\top}
]
.
\triangleright

h
is applied elementwise

return

-N^{t}/(N-1)^{t+1}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\bm{\mathcal{Q}}_{i,j},\quad\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathbf{0}}_{t+1}
.

B.3.
\mathsf{GT}
term by term estimator of the asymptotic variance

In this section we derive the
\mathsf{GT}
counterpart of the term by term estimator (
4.17
). Define for all
t>0

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{b,t}(K^{1}_{t},K^{2}_{t})

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{\mathsf{GT}}\big[\mathrm{I}_{b,t}(K^{1}_{0:t},K^{2}_{0:t})\big|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t},K^{1}_{t},K^{2}_{t}\big],

(S2.4)

and
\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{b,0}(K^{1}_{0},K^{2}_{0}):=\mathbbm{1}_{K^{1}_{0}\neq K^{2}_{0},b_{0}=0}+\mathbbm{1}_{K^{1}_{0}=K^{2}_{0},b_{0}=1}
.

By the tower property and the definition of
\mathbb{E}_{\mathsf{GT}}[\cdot|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t-1}]
, for all
(k,\ell)\in[N]^{2}
and
t>0
, if
b_{t}=0
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{b,t}(k,\ell)

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{A^{k}_{t}=i,A^{\ell}_{t}=j}\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{b,t-1}(i,j)=\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{b,t-1}(A^{k}_{t-1},A^{\ell}_{t-1}),

and if
b_{t}=1
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{b,t}(k,\ell)

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{k=\ell}\sum_{i,j\in[N]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{A^{k}_{t-1}=i}\mathcal{W}^{j}_{t-1}\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{b,t-1}(i,j)=\mathbbm{1}_{k=\ell}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathcal{W}^{j}_{t-1}\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{b,t-1}(A^{k}_{t-1},j).

Similarly to
\mathsf{BS}
, we have by the tower property

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{N,\mathsf{BS}}_{b,t}(h)

\displaystyle=\prod_{s=0}^{t}N^{b_{s}}\bigg(\frac{N}{N-1}\bigg)^{1-b_{s}}\frac{\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}{N^{2}}\sum_{k,\ell\in[N]^{2}}\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{b,t}(k,\ell)h(\xi^{k}_{t},\xi^{\ell}_{t}),

(S2.5)

and the term by term estimator is thus

\overline{\mathcal{V}}^{N,\mathsf{GT}}_{\gamma,t}(h)=\frac{N^{t-1}\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}}{(N-1)^{t}}\sum_{k,\ell\in[N]^{2}}\left\{S^{\mathsf{GT}}_{t}(k,\ell)-\frac{t+1}{N-1}\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{\mathbf{0},t}(k,\ell)\right\}h(\xi^{k}_{t})h(\xi^{\ell}_{t}),

(S2.6)

where
S^{\mathsf{GT}}_{t}
is such that for all
(k,\ell)\in[N]^{2}
,

\displaystyle S^{\mathsf{GT}}_{t}(k,\ell)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{e_{s},t}(k,\ell)=\mathbbm{1}_{k=\ell}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathcal{W}^{j}_{t-1}\mathcal{T}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{\mathbf{0},t-1}(A^{k}_{t-1},j)+\mathbbm{1}_{k\neq\ell}S^{\mathsf{GT}}_{t-1}(A^{k}_{t-1},A^{\ell}_{t-1}),

which shows that (
S2.6
) is also updated online in a rather simple way by propagating the matrices
\bm{S}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{t}
and
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\mathsf{GT}}_{\mathbf{0},t}
.

Appendix C
Technical results

Theorem C.1

(
Generalized dominated convergence theorem
)
.

Let
(f_{N})_{N\in\mathbb{N}}
be a sequence of
\mathcal{X}
-measurable functions and
(g_{N})_{N\in\mathbb{N}}
a sequence of non-negative
\mathcal{X}
-measurable functions.
Assume that the following assumptions hold.

(i)

There exists
C>0
such that
\left|f_{N}(x)\right|\leq Cg_{N}(x)
for all
N\in\mathbb{N}
and
x\in\mathsf{X}
.

(ii)

(g_{N})_{N\in\mathbb{N}}
converges pointwise to
g
and
\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\int g_{N}\mathrm{d}\nu=\int g\mathrm{d}\nu<\infty
.

(iii)

(f_{N})_{N\in\mathbb{N}}
converges pointwise to
f
.

Then,
f
is
\nu
-integrable and
\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\int f_{N}\mathrm{d}\nu=\int f\mathrm{d}\nu
.

Proof.

The proof can be found in
[
36
]
.
∎

Theorem
C.2
and Lemma
C.3
are borrowed from
[
33
]
and
[
15
]
respectively.

Theorem C.2
.

Assume that
(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B}:\ref *{assp:boundup}})
hold. Then, for all
s\in\mathbb{N},

h_{s}\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X}^{\otimes{s+1}})
and
\big(f_{s},\tilde{f}_{s}\big)\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X})^{2}
, there exist constants
\big(C_{s},\widetilde{C}_{s}\big)\in\left(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{*}\right)^{2}
, depending on
h_{s},f_{s}
, and
\tilde{f}_{s}
, such that for all
N\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and all
\varepsilon\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{*}
,

\mathbb{P}\left(\bigg|N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\omega^{i}_{s}\big\{\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{s}]f_{s}\big)(\xi^{i}_{s})+\tilde{f}_{s}\big(\xi^{i}_{s}\big)\big\}-\eta_{s}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{s}]f_{s}+\tilde{f}_{s}\big)\bigg|\geq\varepsilon\right)\leq C_{s}\exp\left(-\widetilde{C}_{s}N\varepsilon^{2}\right),

(S3.1)

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathcal{W}^{i}_{s}\left\{\left(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}^{N}[h_{s}]f_{s}\right)(\xi^{i}_{s})+\tilde{f}_{s}\left(\xi^{i}_{s}\right)\right\}-\phi_{s}\left(\mathrm{\mathbf{T}}_{s}[h_{s}]f_{s}+\tilde{f}_{s}\right)\right|\geq\varepsilon\right)\leq C_{s}\exp\left(-\widetilde{C}_{s}N\varepsilon^{2}\right).

(S3.2)

Lemma C.3
.

Assume that
a_{N},b_{N}
and
b
are random variables defined on the same probability space such that there exist positive constants
\beta,B_{1},C_{1},B_{2},C_{2}
and
M
satisfying the following assumptions.

•

\left|a_{N}/b_{N}\right|\leq M,

\mathbb{P}-a.s.
and
b\geq\beta
.

•

For all
\varepsilon>0
and all
N\geq 1,\mathbb{P}\left(\left|b_{N}-b\right|>\varepsilon\right)\leq B_{1}\exp(-C_{1}N\varepsilon^{2})
.

•

For all
\varepsilon>0
and all
N\geq 1,\mathbb{P}\left(\left|a_{N}\right|>\varepsilon\right)\leq B_{2}\exp(-C_{2}N\varepsilon^{2})
.

Then, there exist two positive constants
B_{3},C_{3}
such that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\frac{a_{N}}{b_{N}}\right|>\varepsilon\right)\leq B_{3}\exp(-C_{3}N\varepsilon^{2}).

Appendix D
Asymptotic variance of the joint predictive distribution

In this section we provide some intuition on (
3.7
). Let
h\in\mathbb{F}_{b}(\mathcal{X})
.
By the law of total variance,

\mathbb{V}\big[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)\big]=\mathbb{V}\big[\mathbb{E}\big[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)\mid\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t}\big]\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{V}[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)\mid\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t}\big]\big].

(S4.1)

As
\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(\mathbf{1})
is
\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t}
-measurable and the particles at time
t+1
are i.i.d conditionally on
\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)\mid\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t}\big]

\displaystyle=\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\omega^{i}_{t}}{\Omega_{t}}\mathrm{M}_{t+1}[h](\xi^{i}_{t})

\displaystyle=\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})N^{-1}\Omega_{t}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\omega^{i}_{t}}{\Omega_{t}}\mathrm{M}_{t+1}[h](\xi^{i}_{t})=\gamma_{t}^{N}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t+1}[h]\big).

(S4.2)

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}\big[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}[h]\mid\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t}\big]

\displaystyle=\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}\mathbb{V}\bigg[\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}h(\xi^{i}_{t+1})\bigg|\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t}\bigg]=N^{-1}\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}\mathbb{V}_{\phi_{t}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t+1}}\big[h(\xi_{t+1})\big]

where

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}_{\phi_{t}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t+1}}\big[h(\xi_{t+1})\big]=\phi_{t}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t+1}\big(\big\{h-\phi_{t}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t+1}(h)\big\}^{2}\big).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}\big[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)\mid\mathcal{F}^{N}_{t}\big]

\displaystyle=N^{-2}\gamma_{t}^{N}(\mathbf{1})^{2}\Omega_{t}\eta_{t}^{N}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t+1}\big[\big\{h-\phi_{t}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t+1}(h)\big\}^{2}\big]\big)

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{t}^{N}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t+1}\big[\big\{h-\phi_{t}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t+1}(h)\big\}^{2}\big]\big).

(S4.3)

Replacing (
D
)-(
D
) in (
S4.1
), we get the recursive formula

\displaystyle N\mathbb{V}\big[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)\big]

\displaystyle=N\mathbb{V}\big[\gamma_{t}^{N}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t+1}[h]\big)\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{t}^{N}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{t+1}\big[\big\{h-\phi_{t}^{N}\mathrm{M}_{t+1}(h)\big\}^{2}\big]\big)\big]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{M}_{0}\big(\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{1:t+1}[h]^{2}\big)-\gamma_{t+1}(h)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 36.98866pt+\sum_{s=1}^{t+1}\mathbb{E}\big[\gamma_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s-1}^{N}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s}\big[\big\{\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]-\phi_{s-1}^{N}\big(\mathrm{M}_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]\big]\big)\big\}^{2}\big]\big)\big].

With multiple applications of (
3.5
) in the main paper, we get that

\gamma_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s-1}^{N}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s}\big[\big\{\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]-\phi_{s-1}^{N}\big(\mathrm{M}_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]\big]\big)\big\}^{2}\big]\big)\\
\overset{a.s.}{\longrightarrow}\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s}\big[\big\{\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]-\phi_{s-1}\big(\mathrm{M}_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]\big]\big)\big\}^{2}\big]\big),

(S4.4)

and, using that
\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\phi_{s-1}(\mathrm{M}_{s}[h])=\gamma_{s}(h)
and
\gamma_{s}\big(\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]\big)=\gamma_{t+1}(h)
for all
h
we get

\displaystyle\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s-1}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s}\big[\big\{\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]

\displaystyle-\phi_{s-1}\big(\mathrm{M}_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]\big]\big)\big\}^{2}\big]\big)

\displaystyle=\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s}\big(\big\{\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]-\phi_{s-1}\big(\mathrm{M}_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]\big]\big)\big\}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle=\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}(h)^{2}\big]-\gamma_{t+1}(h)^{2}.

Finally, by
(\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{\ref *{assp:B})}}
and the boundedness of
h
,
|\gamma_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})|\leq G_{\infty}^{s}
,
|\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t}[h]|\leq G_{\infty}^{t-s}|h|_{\infty}
, thus

\big|\gamma_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s-1}^{N}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s}\big[\big\{\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]-\phi_{s-1}^{N}\big(\mathrm{M}_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]\big]\big)\big\}^{2}\big]\big)\big|\leq 4G_{\infty}^{2t}|h|_{\infty},

and by the dominated convergence theorem, for any
s\in[0:t]
,

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\big[\gamma_{s}^{N}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s-1}^{N}\big(\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}_{s}\big[\big\{\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]-\phi_{s-1}^{N}\big(\mathrm{M}_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}[h]\big]\big)\big\}^{2}\big]\big)\big]\\
=\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}(h)^{2}\big]-\gamma_{t+1}(h)^{2}.

(S4.5)

and
N\mathbb{V}\big[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}[h]\big]\longrightarrow\sum_{s=0}^{t+1}\big\{\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}(h)^{2}\big]-\gamma_{t+1}(h)^{2}\big\}
. As argued in Section
4.3
of the main paper,
\mathbb{E}\big[N\big(\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)-\gamma_{t+1}(h)\big)^{2}\big]
converges to the asymptotic variance when
N
goes to infinity, and since
\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)
is an unbiased estimator of
\gamma_{t+1}(h)
,
N\mathbb{V}\big[\gamma_{t+1}^{N}[h]\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[N\big(\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)-\gamma_{t+1}(h)\big)^{2}\big]
. Therefore,

\underset{N\to\infty}{\lim}\mathbb{E}\big[N\big(\gamma_{t+1}^{N}(h)-\gamma_{t+1}(h)\big)^{2}\big]=\sum_{s=0}^{t+1}\big\{\gamma_{s}(\mathbf{1})\gamma_{s}\big[\overline{\mathrm{\mathbf{Q}}}_{s+1:t+1}(h)^{2}\big]-\gamma_{t+1}(h)^{2}\big\},

which ends the proof.
